# Crawlers:Abandoned cart
Abandoned cart
Abandoned cart is a dungeon event in Vampire Crawlers.
Destroying the cart will create an icon of a bracer. Walk into it again to collect it. 
Once collected, it will add +1 to hand for the duration of the run.
## Cart
 - 
 [Undamaged]
 Undamaged
 - 
 [1 hit]
 1 hit
 - 
 [2 hits]
 2 hits
 - 
 [3 hits]
 3 hits
 -

## Audio
- "Time for adventure."
- "Let it whip."

- "Fight to the bitter end."

## Character bio
This section contains info obtained from datamining and may not currently be available in-game.
"Armed with his trusty whip and a jawline that could cut garlic, Antonio is the boldest (and most accident-prone) of the monster-slaying Belpaeses. Unlike his ancestors, who had years of rigorous training, his primary qualifications include looking good in a billowy shirt and swinging wildly until the problem goes away. If anything takes him down, it’ll be his own stupidity—not the undead."

## Character bio
This section contains info obtained from datamining and may not currently be available in-game.
"Born from scandal, raised in spite, and dramatically brooding ever since, Arca clings to the idea that he’s of noble vampiric descent. His only proof? A filthy, barely legible note that smells suspiciously like pigsty. With a name that sounds important and firepower to match, he’s determined to burn his way to respect—one overcompensating fireball at a time."

# Crawlers:Arcanas
Arcanas
Arcanas in Vampire Crawlers are modifier cards played from the Fortune Teller before entering a dungeon that affects the rest of the deck during the dungeon run.
## List of arcana
| Sprite | Name | Card text | Description | Unlocked by
| | And Another | Repeated cards this turn are Free. | Cards are Free-to-Play if a version of it has been played this turn. | Playing 150 Free-to-Play cards.
| | Chain Link | Combos don't clear between turns. | Combos don't clear between turns. | Reaching at least a combo of 12 or more.
| | Experimental Medicine | Double healing. | Doubles any healing effects. | Unlocked when taking the Polentír
| | Jester's Hat | Add a Wild card each floor. | Add a Wild card to your Deck at the start of each floor. | Playing a total of 150 Wild cards.
| | Make a Scene | Trigger abilities at start of turn. | Activate Crawler trigger abilities for free at the start of each turn. | Activating 1,500 Crawler trigger abilities.
| | Mana Syphon | Increases Mana every 100 cards. | Permanently increase Mana after every 100 cards played. | Receiving 15 Mana in a single turn.
| | Over The Top | Move Crawlers to the top of your Deck. | When a Crawler leaves, put them at the top of your Deck. | Using crawler cards (green character cards) 100 times.
| | Sharp Mind | Keep up to 5 Mana between turns. | Keep up to 5 Mana between turns. | Using purple cards (mana-adding cards) 100 times.
| | Shield Bash | Deal Armor as damage when played. | Deal your total armor value as damage to the front row after playing an Armor card. | Unlocked when taking the Polentír
| | Swollen Fist | Deal Deck Pile as damage after turn. | Deal damage based on the size of your Deck Pile at the end of each turn. | Reaching 40 Deck size.
| | Wild Buff | Cards picked up from light sources get combo multipliers. | Cards picked up from light sources now benefit from combo multipliers. | Playing 200 cards picked up from light sources.
| | Your Shield My Liege | Armor persists. | Remaining Armor persists between turns. | Gaining 2,000 Armor.

# Crawlers:Area
Area
This article is about the stat. For the gem, see Crawlers:Area (gem).
Area is one of the deck stats in Vampire Crawlers. It is capped at 1,000% (in a single battle) and starts at a base of 0.
## PowerUp
| Area
| Attacks deal splash damage. 10% per rank.
| Dungeon
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| ID | PowerUp_Area
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | +10%
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +50%
| Unlocked by | Accessing Power Ups shop
| Cost | 850
| {"description":"Attacks deal splash damage. 10% per rank.","cost":"850","id":"PowerUp_Area","unlocked by":"Accessing Power Ups shop","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","image":"","name":"Area","max effect":"+50%","release":"21 April 2026","stacking":"Additive","max level":"5","bonus":"+10%"}
The Area Power Up increases the amount of splash damage attacks will deal: Every 5% area adds 1% bonus damage to every enemy in front.
- Additionally more noticeably, the Area increases the size of projectiles thus increasing the chances of spell / attack cards hitting enemies, most noticeably being the case with Runetracer and NO FUTURE which have the damage determined as "RNG". In case of Gatti Amari the increased size also increases the collision chance to trigger the Scuffle effect.
=====

Axe

| {"type":"attack","cost":"2","id":"Card_A_3_Axe","text":"Deal 45 damage to multiple enemies with a chance to Knockback.","unlock":"Available by default.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","evointo":"Death Spiral","image":"","name":"Axe","max gem slots":"3","release":"21 April 2026","gem slots":"1","demo":"Yes","evointogroup":"Candle"}
Axe is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers. It starts with 1 gem slot and is unlocked by default. The minimum level required for this card to appear in level up reward selection is 11.
It can be evolved into  Death Spiral with any card in the  Candle group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Axe has a base  Amount of 3, with each projectile dealing a base damage of 15 to up to two enemies. It targets enemies sequentially from right to left, starting from the front row. Each projectile will hit an enemy and the enemy to its right (wrapping around to the far left for the enemy on the furthest right); the next projectile thrown will not target an enemy hit by the previous projectile. If there is only a single enemy, each projectile will only hit once. Any projectiles in excess of the number of enemies in a row once the rightmost enemy is reached starts again from the right in that same row. Only once there are no enemies remaining in a row will it aim at the row behind.
=====

# Crawlers:Bat Goblin
Bat Goblin is a dungeon event in Vampire Crawlers. In this event, the goal of the player is to throw as many of their cards onto the Bat Goblin as they can to collect experience gems.
## Tips
- Disabling Hurry Mode would allow the player to have more time to throw more cards at the Bat Goblin, thus be able to collect more experience gems.

# Crawlers:Blacksmith
| Blacksmith
| In the Blacksmith you can modify Cards and Gems.
| Building
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Function | Allows for adding gem slots in Blacksmith and modifying gem rarities in Jeweller.
| Unlocking | Obtaining Stardust Anvil for Blacksmith only and obtaining Lapidary Loupe for Jeweller.
| Music | Never Too Prepared (Blacksmith)
| Chronology
| ← Previous | Current | Next →
| World Map | Blacksmith | Museum
| {"description":"In the Blacksmith you can modify Cards and Gems.","next":"Museum","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","function":"Allows for adding gem slots in Blacksmith and modifying gem rarities in Jeweller.","image":"","name":"Blacksmith","prev":"World Map","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Never Too Prepared (Blacksmith)","unlocking":"Obtaining Stardust Anvil for Blacksmith only and obtaining Lapidary Loupe for Jeweller."}
The Blacksmith's Workshop, or simply the Blacksmith, is one of the eight buildings that can be found in the Village. It has two functions:
- It allows for adding gem slots in Blacksmith.
- It allows for modifying rarities of gems in Jeweller.
The Blacksmith's shop is unlocked after obtaining the  Stardust Anvil. The Jeweller's shop is unlocked by obtaining the  Lapidary Loupe.
## Blacksmith
TBA
## Jeweller
Gems are classified into five rarity tiers: Common, Uncommon, Rare, Very Rare, and Ultra Rare. See Gems for further details.
Upgrading a gem's rarity beyond Ultra Rare seals the gem, rendering it unavailable from chests or level ups.
The cost of changing gem rarity rises with each successive use and is capped at 50,000 coins.
## Trivia
- It is the only building to have two relics unlock its shops/services from a single building.
- This is one of the three buildings to be introduced in the 1.0 release of the game. The other two are the Museum and the Fortune Teller.
- It is one of the two buildings that are unlocked by obtaining a relic, with the other being the Fortune Teller.
=====

On Crit: Heal 2.","release":"21 April 2026","demo":"Yes","max gem slots":"2","gem slots":"1"}
Bloody Tear is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers. It is unlocked by evolving the  Whip.
It is evolved from  Whip with any card in the  Hollow Heart group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Bloody Tear has a base  Amount of 10, with each projectile dealing a base Damage of 4 to every enemy which does not have another enemy in front of it.
There is a chance for each instance of Damage (rather than each projectile or each projectile per enemy) to instead be a critical hit, hitting for 2x Damage on that instance (e.g. increasing to 8 if there are no other modifiers).
If there is at least one instance of a critical hit when a Blood Tear is played, then 2 Health is restored. The amount of Health restored does not increase if there is more than one critical hit.

# Crawlers:Bomba Infernale
| Bomba Infernale
| Instantly vaporise all enemies on the first floor of a dungeon you have previously cleared.
| Relic
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| ID | RelicConfig_BombaInfernale
| Effects | Adds a button that kills all Enemies on the first floor of a previously completed Dungeon and collects their XP.
| Found in | Dairy Plant
| {"foundin":"Dairy Plant","image":"","description":"Instantly vaporise all enemies on the first floor of a dungeon you have previously cleared.","name":"Bomba Infernale","release":"21 April 2026","id":"RelicConfig_BombaInfernale","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","effects":"Adds a button that kills all Enemies on the first floor of a previously completed Dungeon and collects their XP."}
Bomba Infernale is a relic in Vampire Crawlers. It can be found in Dairy Plant.
## Mechanics
When a player completes a specific dungeon and then revisits it, a red button will appear only on the first floor of the dungeon, which can be opened and then used by the player. Once the button is pressed, a large explosion will occur, killing all of the enemies present in the floor, and automatically collecting all of their experience gems. Afterwards, only treasure chests and various events would be left to be optionally encountered, though the treasure chest awarded by killing the boss on the first floor will not appear.

# Crawlers:Card effects
Card effects in Vampire Crawlers are special behaviors found on cards. They are often denoted by a yellow keyword describing the effect, and a longer description of the effect when hovering over the card.
## List of keywords
## Big Bird
"Disperse all active birds, dealing increased damage."
## Countdown
## Crawler
This keyword is only present on character cards, and describes how the character interacts with other cards played while they are active. Each Crawler effect is unique, lasting a different base  Duration and triggering its effect based on playing different card types.
## Crit
## Destroy
"Card burns up on use."
Destroy will cause the card to be lost after it has been played. When a card with this effect is played into a combo stack, it is not triggered until the stack is emptied. Consequentially, if the  Fireproof gem is slotted into a card in the combo stack, then the card with this effect will not be destroyed.
## Disarm
"High chance of preventing a single enemy attack."
Disarm works very similar to Knockback, but only affects a single enemy, and is affected by the enemy's disarm resistance rather than their knockback resistance.
## Disarm*
"High chance of preventing 2 enemy attacks."
Disarm works very similar to Knockback, but only affects up to 2 enemies, and is affected by the enemy's disarm resistance rather than their knockback resistance.
## Free
"Card won't consume any Mana when played."
The Free keyword isn't printed onto the card text like most other effect keywords. Instead it appears above the mana cost on newly-obtained cards gained from level up during an encounter.
## Knockback
"Prevents an enemy from attacking next turn."
Knockback has a chance of knocking an enemy back, which is affected by each enemy's individual knockback resistance. If an enemy gets affected by the knockback effect, they will no longer attack during that turn and need to move forward once the player's turn ends.
## Little Heart
## Mana Scaling
## Temporary
## Raw Mana
## Scuffle

# Crawlers:Card stat offering table
Card stat offering table or Bing Upgrades Vending Machine, is a dungeon event in Vampire Crawlers.
Interacting with the Card stat offering table will open a menu containing stat boost cards in the player's deck. Hovering over one of these passives will show a buff to it's corresponding power up. Selecting a card will destroy it and add the buff for the duration of the run. 
Players may skip the event by either not running into it or pressing the Cash out button to gain 200 coins.
## List of upgrades
| Card | Upgrade
| Bracer | +1 Hand
| Parm Aegis | +1 Armor
| Armor | +1 Armor
| Golden Armor | +2 Armor
| Rainbow Armor | +3 Armor
| Spinach | +10% Might
| Sprig o' Spinach | +20% Might
| Candle | +5% Area
| Candella | +10% Area
| Candelabrador | +15% Area
| Duplicator | +1 Amount
| Du-Duplicator | +2 Amount
| Tirajisú | +10% Revival
| Clover Petal | +10% Luck
| Little Clover | +20% Luck
| Clover | +20% Luck
| Crown | +30% Growth
| Crystal Crown | +40% Growth
| Stone Mask | +20% Greed
| Skull O'Maniac | +5% Curse

# Crawlers:Cards
Cards are the core mechanic of Vampire Crawlers, with each representing a different action the player can take while in a dungeon. They are colored based on which type of card they are, with teal being characters, red being attacks, blue being defenses, yellow being stat boosts, purple being mana generators, white being wild cards, and black being temporary cards.
## Damage calculation
Main article: Crawlers:Damage
Damage=(CardDamage+CardDamage*Combo+BaseDamage*Amount)*(1+Might)*(1+Area/5)*GemMultiplier1*GemMultiplier2
Damage: the number displayed on enemies.
CardDamage: the number displayed on cards while not in combat.
Combo: the multiplier number when stacking combos.
 Amount: capped at 50.
 Might: capped at 1000%.
 Area: capped at 1000%. Every 5% area adds 1% bonus damage to every enemy in front.
GemMultiplier:  Double Damage and  Triple Damage have this. Stack multiplicatively.
## Sorting
As of 1.5, players can now sort their deck in two ways:
- By Mana: Cards, from left to right, will be listed in descending Mana cost order, then wild cards will be listed to the far right. For example, if among the cards in hand, the highest Mana cost is 3, cards with that Mana cost will be listed first, then ones with a Mana cost of 2 next to them, and so on. If the player has any wild cards, those will be listed next to the card(s) with the lowest Mana cost.
- By Color: From left to right, cards are sorted this way: attack cards (red, including evolved weapon cards), stat boost cards (yellow), defense cards (blue), mana cards (purple), crawler cards (green), wild cards (gray).
If the player receives a new card in hand, it will by unsorted by default. Furthermore, once the player receives a batch of cards after the enemies' turn, the batch of cards will be unsorted.

# Crawlers:Characters
Characters
The Gorton Bell Inn, in the Village.
Disco mode activated in the Gorton Bell Inn.
Characters or Crawlers are the character cards in Vampire Crawlers. Players can purchase and equip characters through the Gorton Bell Inn.
The leading main (first chosen) crawler adds their own parametres / stats / power ups to the party total (E.G. Antonio adds +10 Max Health). The leading main crawler also adds four (cards) to the starter deck. Any following crawler (from Gorton Bell and recruited within dungeons) adds no additional power ups, and only one of their attack / spell cards is added to the starter deck (E.G. non-leading Poppea adds only one Song of Mana to the starter deck and no other cards).
Each character has their own unique Crawler effect on their card, which is typically triggered by playing a particular color card, and lasts for a set amount, increased by the  Duration deck stat. A higher Duration stat adds increases the initial amount to the character's Crawler effect trigger amount.
Once 5 Crawlers have been purchased, there is a 10% chance to trigger a "Disco mode" in the Inn. During Disco mode, a 5% discount is applied to additional purchases of Crawlers. The Disco mode ends when leaving the Inn. And requires a dungeon run in order to be eligible for a chance of re-activation.

# Crawlers:Coin
Gold Coin (currency)
Coins are the currency in Vampire Crawlers.
Coins can be obtained through light sources, completing dungeons, cashing out chests, or by other means.
Coins can be spent on new Crawlers in the Inn or by recruiting new Crawlers in a dungeon, upgrading Power Ups in the Shop, adding gem slots in Cards from the Blacksmith, or by changing gem rarity in the Jeweller.
## Modifiers
The following items add or involve coins.
## Cards
-  Gallo gains 25% more coins. Playing a wild card with Gallo will add 10% more coins.
-  Vicious Hunger can turn enemies into coins when killed.
-  Stone Mask gains 10% more coins when used.
-  Big Coin Bag gives the player 25 more coins upon usage.
-  Coin Purse gives the player 10 more coins upon usage.
-  Rich Coin Bag gives the player more coins upon usage.
## Relics
-  Overkill allows the player to earn extra coins by dealing extra damage to boss enemies.
-  Ultimate Ultra Overkill amplifies the number of extra coins earned.
## Gems
-  Coin Count can damage an enemy based on the number of coins the player has while in a dungeon.
-  Destroy destroys the card that it is slotted in, but in return, the player gains extra coins.
-  Midas makes the player earn coins when the card(s) that it is slotted in is/are in the player's hand.
-  Mug makes the player earn coins from an enemy that it kills.
-  Greed increases Greed stat, which in turn, increases the amount of earn coins.
- Although not directly involving coins themselves,  Coin Card creates  Big Coin Bag cards, which in turn add coins upon their usage.
## Power Ups/Stats
-  Greed increases the percentage of coins gained by the player in most cases.
## Unlocks
The following items are unlocked by obtaining a certain total number of coins.
- Power Ups Shop is unlocked by collecting 250 coins.
-  Mug is unlocked by collecting 3,333 coins.
-  Dommario is unlocked by collecting 5,000 coins.
-  Midas is unlocked by collecting 5,555 coins.
-  Coin Count is unlocked by collecting 100,000 coins.
=====

# Crawlers:Coin Count
| Coin Count
| 
| Gem
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| In demo | No
| Rarity | Ultra Rare
| Card text | Deal damage based on number of coins.

| {"unlocked by":"Collecting 100,000 coins.","image":"","name":"Coin Count","rarity":"Ultra Rare","release":"21 April 2026","text":"Deal damage based on number of coins.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","demo":"No"}
Coin Count is a gem in Vampire Crawlers. 
When a card with this gem socketed is played, one random enemy will receive damage equal to the number of coins the player has collected during the run. 
- This damage is not affected by gems such as Double Damage and Triple Damage. However, slotting multiple Coin Count gems on the same card can allow it to deal more damage, though this is not displayed in-game.

# Crawlers:Combo
Combo is the core mechanism in Vampire Crawlers.
It is available after obtaining  Combo Stack.
Playing cards in ascending order of mana cost alongside  Wild cards increase the combo multiplier stack.
- For an XX‑combo, the effect is equivalent to playing the card XX additional times repeatedly. Combo only receives benefits from  Might,  Area,  Double Damage,  Triple Damage and  Leader.
The combo multiplier will be displayed at the bottom‑right corner of the screen.
=====
# Crawlers:Combo Breaker
| Combo Breaker
| 
| Gem
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| In demo | Yes
| Rarity | Very Rare
| Card text | Consumes the combo to benefit from the multiplier.
| Unlocked by | Reaching 5 Combo or above.
| {"unlocked by":"Reaching 5 Combo or above.","image":"","name":"Combo Breaker","rarity":"Very Rare","release":"21 April 2026","text":"Consumes the combo to benefit from the multiplier.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","demo":"Yes"}
Combo Breaker, previously known as Easy Combo, is a gem in Vampire Crawlers.
It allows the slotted card to benefit from the combo streak when played out of order (E.G. playing an Combo Breaker slotted card of 1-Mana cost onto 5-Mana cost card). This action however will break / reset the combo streak. In order to preserve combo streak, a  Wild gem is recommended instead.
=====

## Trivia
- Concetta's voice lines contain several references to famous songs/lyrics/quotes.
- "Music for the neck downwards." - A quote attributed to guitarist and The Rolling Stones member Keith Richards.
- "Win some lose some, it's all the same to me" - Ace of Spades, by Motörhead.
- "Looking for adventure." - Born to Be Wild by Steppenwolf.
- "It's better to burn out than fade away." - My My, Hey Hey (Out of the Blue) by Neil Young.
- "Put another dime in the jukebox baby." - I Love Rock 'n' Roll, famously covered by Joan Jett.
- "Nothing else matters." - Nothing Else Matters, 1992 song by Metallica.
- "Gotta get away." - Gotta Get Away, 1995 song by The Offspring.

# Crawlers:Crawler Slot
Crawler Slot is one of the deck stats in Vampire Crawlers. It is capped at 3 Characters and starts at a base of 1.
Crawler Slot determines the number of initial Crawlers / Characters one can embark to a dungeon crawl.
There is otherwise no upper limit / cap on how many Crawlers one can have active during a dungeon run, allowing players to recruit more party members from Outhouses.

- Milk Factory:  Poppea
- Curd Refinery:  Ultimate Ultra Overkill

# Crawlers:Damage
Damage is the number displayed on enemies when using a card. Damage can not be higher than the enemy's health left.
## Damage calculation
Damage=(CardDamage+CardDamage*Combo+BaseDamage*Amount)*(1+Might)*(1+Area/5)*GemMultiplier1*GemMultiplier2
Damage: the number displayed on enemies.
CardDamage: the number displayed on cards while not in combat.
Combo: the multiplier number when stacking combos.
 Amount: capped at 50.
 Might: capped at 1000%.
 Area: capped at 1000%. Every 5% area adds 1% bonus damage to every enemy in front.
GemMultiplier:  Double Damage and  Triple Damage have this. Stack multiplicatively.
Since Combo and  Amount share the same multiplier bucket,  Amount is valuable at low Combo multiplier, while its effectiveness diminishes as Combo multiplier stacks.
## Examples
 Knife card damage=40, Combo=25, base damage=8,  Amount=50,  Might=1000%,  Area=1000%, with 2  Triple Damage.
damage=(40+40*25+8*50)*(1+1000%)*(1+1000%/5)*3*3=427,680
=====

| {"type":"attack","cost":"3","id":"Card_A_3_DeathSpiral","unlocked by":"Evolving Axe.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","evofromgroup":"Candle","evofrom":"Axe","image":"","name":"Death Spiral","text":"Deal 160 damage to the front row.Crit.","release":"21 April 2026","demo":"Yes","max gem slots":"3","gem slots":"1"}
Death Spiral is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers.
It is evolved from  Axe with any card in the  Candle group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Death Spiral has a base  Amount of 16, with each projectile dealing a base Damage of 10 to every enemy in the front row. It will not target enemies not in the front row, even enemies with no enemy in front of them.
There is a chance for each instance of Damage (rather than each projectile or each projectile per enemy) to instead be a critical hit, hitting for 2x Damage on that instance (e.g. increasing to 20 if there are no other modifiers).

- Just outside of the Museum in the Village, there are four statues of Dommario.

# Crawlers:Dungeon events
Dungeon events in Vampire Crawlers are encounters with objects or enemies that provide various bonuses or benefits from interacting with them.
## List of events
## Destructibles
- Abandoned cart
- Evolution statue
- Mana statue
- Light source
## Passives
- Bat Goblin
- Card stat offering table (Colloquially "Bing Upgrades vending machine")
- Floor chicken offering table
- Mana offering table
- Outhouse
- Duplicate offering table (Mirror)
- Treasure chest (Internally "Gem chest")
=====
# Crawlers:Dungeons
Stages
Dungeons are the primary locations in Vampire Crawlers, where the player goes to play cards against enemies and discover treasure chests of loot. Each dungeon is divided into multiple stages, each with their own difficulty level and number of floors. The goal within each stage is to find a shovel to dig to the next floor.
Dungeons are accessed from the World Map.
## List of dungeons
| Dungeon | Stage | Difficulty | Floors | Description
| Tutorial | Tutorial | | 1 | 
| Village | Village | | | The village of Gorton, a sleepy, parochial place. Don't let its tranquil exterior fool you. This is a melting pot for heroes and heroines! An adventurer's terminus quo.
| Mad Forest | Mad Forest | 1 | 4 | Once a thriving haven, now a dumping ground for evil. A vampire is said to be the root of this evil, but we can find only mayhem and roast chicken.
| Furious Forest | 2 | 5 | The deeper the forest goes the more unsettling the monsters become. Something in the air must be pushing them to insanity. Dark magic or maybe the quality of the chicken.
| Berserk Wood | 3 | 5 | There is no turning back from the depths once inside. A muffled cry can be heard between the trees, along with flapping of a thousand wings.
| Inlaid Library | Inlaid Library | 2 | 5 | The Futile Scribe was cursed to know all and understand nothing. His endless library may gift us insight... or damnation. A mask of stone whispers endlessly within.
| Library West Wing | 3 | 5 | No sane intruder would dare talk louder than a whisper, in this labyrinth of book shelves and cursed tomes. Inhuman forms stalk the halls, seeking long overdue revenge.
| Library Sanctum | 4 | 5 | Once home to some of the world's brightest and most talented mages. Now there is nothing but dark magic, while familiars and those who seek to control them.
| Teeny Bridge | Teeny Bridge | 3 | 1 | This handsome little bridge seems perfectly safe to cross. Definitely better than swimming, anyway.
| Dairy Plant | Dairy Plant | 4 | 5 | The birthplace of Milk Magic churns with chaos. Here, it's not just blood that curdles. A Creamy Surprise awaits the hero brave enough to enter this overthrown factory.
| Milk Factory | 5 | 5 | In days gone by, corrupt officials skimmed off the top to line their pockets. Now all that remains is a maze of pipes, haunted by the huge golems which once protected this place.
| Curd Refinery | 6 | 5 | The epicenter of the Dairy industrial complex, where many a cheesy adventurer has lost their whey. Beware the milk-crazed beasts and raw milky magic.
| Weeny Bridge | Weeny Bridge | 5 | 1 | This bridge seems even smaller and quieter than the last one. No doubt this time it will be safe to cross in peace.
| Gallo Tower | Gallo Tower | 6 | 6 | An edifice of science and sorcery where horror was made manifest in the name of progress. Its many artifacts and experiments may lead us to what we seek.
| Meany Bridge | Meany Bridge | 7 | 1 | An innocent looking bridge with an intimidating name. What could possibly go wrong?
| Cappella Magna | Cappella Magna | 8 | 5 | Conceited were they that ruled from so high yet stooped so low. This nexus of debased purity is the perfect place to find a vampire. Probably.
| Cappella Ultima | 9 | 5 | Here lies the floating throne room. Where the puppet masters of all this land's woes dangle their strings. A fitting perch for those who fancy themselves gods yet wallow in rot.
| ??? | ??? | 0 | 1 | What is this beneath the waves... Just an NPC encounter, or perhaps a sneaky preview of future content?
## Modes
There are a couple of Dungeon modes that can be enabled or disabled in the World Map or the Museum, if the player has unlocked their corresponding relics.
## Hurry Mode
Hurry Mode speeds up the general gameplay in dungeons, including player speed, player attacks, and enemy attacks. The time spent in dungeon is doubled, E.G. 2-minutes in real-life is marked as 4-minutes in game. This mode becomes available after unlocking the  Sorceress' Tears, which can be found in Gallo Tower.
## Endless Mode
Endless Mode is a mode introduced in 1.5. It allows for players to explore an unlimited number of floors, with increasing enemy difficulty, for dungeons that they have previously completed. The modifications from the Jeweller are disabled in this mode.
This mode becomes available after unlocking the  Seventh Trumpet, which can be found in ???[sic].
=====
# Crawlers:Duplicate offering table
Duplicate offering table is a dungeon event in Vampire Crawlers. It resembles a mirror and allows the player to duplicate one of their existing cards.

# Crawlers:Duration
Duration
This article is about the stat. For the gem, see Crawlers:Duration (gem).
Duration is one of the deck stats in Vampire Crawlers. It adds additional initial counts of Crawler ability triggers when their summon card is played, For example, with Duration at 5, Antonio has 5 + 5 triggers when summoned.
While in battles this Duration itself cannot be increased, it does not have a set cap, while also starting at a base of 0.
## PowerUp
| Duration
| Increase Duration Crawlers stay by 1 per rank.
| Dungeon
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| ID | PowerUp_Duration
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | +1
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +5
| Unlocked by | Accessing Power Ups shop
| Cost | 250

# Crawlers:Evolution
Evolution
Not to be confused with Crawlers:Evolution statue.
| Evolution
| 
| Gem
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| In demo | Yes
| Rarity | Ultra Rare
| Card text | Evolves the card into a more powerful weapon.
| Unlocked by | Unlocked when taking the Gem Hammer
| {"unlocked by":"Unlocked when taking the Gem Hammer","image":"","name":"Evolution","rarity":"Ultra Rare","release":"21 April 2026","text":"Evolves the card into a more powerful weapon.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","demo":"Yes"}
Evolution is a gem in Vampire Crawlers. It will only appear if the player has a specific combination of cards in their deck. In some cases, the secondary card can be any card from a group of similar cards. Both the primary and secondary card get consumed, resulting in just the evolution being added to the deck.
The primary card selected needs to have an available gem slot for the Evolution gem to be inserted into. Any remaining gems on the primary card will be inherited by the evolution, but the gems in the secondary, and tertiary, cards will be lost when the primary card is evolved.
If one attempts to earn an Evolution gem from the evolution statue without having valid cards for an evolution, it will instead offer evolution fulfilment components such as a Whip for Hollow Heart or vice versa.
## List of evolutions
| Primary | Secondary | Evolution
| Whip | Hollow Heart Forever Heart | Bloody Tear
| Axe | Candle Candella Candelabrador | Death Spiral
| Pentagram | Crown Crystal Crown | Gorgeous Moon
| Cross | Clover | Heaven Sword
| Fire Wand | Spinach Sprig o' Spinach | Hellfire
| Magic Wand | Empty Tome Light Tome Weighty Tome Ancient Tome | Holy Wand
| Santa Water | Attractorb | La Borra
| Song of Mana | Skull O'Maniac | Mannajja
| Runetracer | Armor Golden Armor Rainbow Armor Hero's Armor | NO FUTURE
| Eight The Sparrow Phiera Der Tuphello | Tirajisú | Phieraggi
| Garlic | Pummarola Pummadora | Soul Eater
| Knife | Bracer | Thousand Edge
| Lightning Ring | Duplicator Du-Duplicator | Thunder Loop
| King Bible | Spellbinder | Unholy Vespers
| Shadow Pinion | Wings | Valkyrie Turner
| Peachone Ebony Wings | Vandalier
| Gatti Amari | Stone Mask | Vicious Hunger
=====
# Crawlers:Evolution statue
Evolution statue is a dungeon event in Vampire Crawlers. It will appear as a solid black block of marble which can be smashed into multiple times to reveal the statue and interacted with.
Interacting with the revealed statue opens a menu containing up to three different weapon evolution choices. The selected choice will have an evolution gem slotted into it.
Like evolving weapons through chests or a gem found in the level up screen, a secondary needs to be sacrificed in order to complete the evolution. Backing out of the passive sacrifice screen does not consume the gem in the evolution statue.
If the current deck does not contain a valid evolution pair (and there is an available gem slot for an evolution stone), the statue will instead offer other evolution component cards to select from.
If there are no available gem slots for an evolution stone on weapons in the player's deck, they will instead be offered a Big Coin Bag.
The event can be skipped by walking past the evolution statue, or by cashing out for 200 gold.

Fire Wand is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers. It starts with 1 gem slot. The minimum level required for this card to appear in level up reward selection is 1.
It can be evolved into  Hellfire with any card in the  Spinach group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Fire Wand has a base  Amount of 3, with each projectile dealing a base Damage of 15. It targets enemies which do not have an enemy in front of them at random.

# Crawlers:Floor Chicken
Floor Chicken
Floor Chicken is a pickup in Vampire Crawlers that heals a certain amount of HP. It is either sourced from light sources or from the floor chicken offering table.
Picking up a total of 5 Floor Chickens will make the player unlock  Garlic.
## Trivia
- The descriptions of Mad Forest and Furious Forest make a reference to Floor Chicken.

# Crawlers:Floor chicken offering table
Floor chicken offering table is a dungeon event in Vampire Crawlers.
This event allows sacrificing a card for full healing.

Forever Heart is a stat boost card in Vampire Crawlers.
It is unlocked by playing 25 Hollow Heart cards. But due this card a "hidden secret", it is not listed in the Town Hall unlocking method list and thus there is no message popup announcing the unlocking after the qualifying dungeon run / crawl session.
Afterwards, the minimum level required for this card to appear in level up reward selection is 11.

# Crawlers:Fortune Teller
| Fortune Teller
| Step right up for all things Arcana related.
| Building
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Function | Allows for selecting and equipping an Arcana.
| Unlocking | Obtaining the Polentír.
| Music | Never Too Prepared (Arcana)
| Chronology
| ← Previous | Current | Next →
| Inn | Fortune Teller | World Map
| {"description":"Step right up for all things Arcana related.","next":"World Map","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","function":"Allows for selecting and equipping an Arcana.","image":"","name":"Fortune Teller","prev":"Inn","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Never Too Prepared (Arcana)","unlocking":"Obtaining the Polentír."}
The Fortune Teller's Arcana tent, or simply the Fortune Teller, is one of the eight buildings that can be found in the Village. It allows for the player to select and equip an Arcana that they unlocked.
This building is unlocked by obtaining the  Polentír. Upon unlocking this building (or the relic), the player will unlock two Arcanas:
-  Experimental Medicine
-  Shield Bash
## List of arcanas
Main article: Crawlers:Arcanas
The following table indicates the row and column as to where each arcana is placed in this building.
| Arcana placements in Fortune Teller
| And Another | Chain Link | Experimental Medicine | Jester's Hat | Make a Scene
| Mana Syphon | Over The Top | Sharp Mind | Shield Bash | Swollen Fist
| Wild Buff | Your Shield My Liege
## Trivia
- This is one of the three buildings to be introduced in the 1.0 release of the game. The other two are the Museum and the Blacksmith.
- It is one of the two buildings that are unlocked by obtaining a relic, with the other being the Blacksmith.
=====

| {"unlocked by":"Playing 200 Free-to-Play cards.","image":"","name":"Free To Play","rarity":"Ultra Rare","release":"21 April 2026","text":"Card won't consume any Mana when played.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","demo":"No"}
Free To Play is a gem in Vampire Crawlers.
Cards with this gem will not have any mana deducted from the player when used but will continue the combo at their specific mana cost.
## Level Up mechanics
When a player obtains a new card during a level up while fighting a horde enemies, that card will temporarily be marked as "Free to Play" for one round after being obtained from a level up. This effect only applies to the current horde of enemies that a player is fighting, so this "Free to Play" effect would not carry on to the next horde of enemies if the card remained unused, unlike the Free To Play gem that would always be applied to the card(s) that it is slotted to.
In essence, Free To Play gem turns the card into a permanent "Level Up Reward"-card, rather than the no-cost-state being exhausted after usage or battle.
Not be confused with the Wild gem or Wild cards: Playing a Free To Play card out of the consecutive order will break the combo multiplier.

Its many artifacts and experiments may lead us to what we seek.","order":"7","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","difficulty":"6","image":"","name":"Gallo Tower","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Crawling, Turbo","floors":"6","demo":"No"}
Gallo Tower is a dungeon in Vampire Crawlers. It is unlocked by completing Weeny Bridge.
Defeating the  Giant Enemy Crab in this stage will unlock Meany Bridge.
## Unlocks
The following characters and relics can be unlocked from this dungeon:
-  Concetta
-  Lapidary Loupe
-  Sorceress' Tears
## Notes
- Unlike the preceding floors, the final floor of Gallo Tower has a single small room and balcony/bridge, which only has a single boss, Gallo.
## Trivia
- It is currently the dungeon with the most floors in the game.

Garlic is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers. It starts with 1 gem slot. The minimum level required for this card to appear in level up reward selection is 1.
It can be evolved into  Soul Eater with any card in the  Pummarola group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Garlic has a base  Amount of 1, with each projectile dealing a base Damage of 10 to every enemy in the front row. It will not target enemies not in the front row, even enemies with no enemy in front of them.
Its special effect, Disarm, imposes a high chance for enemies in the front row to not attack the player when it becomes their turn to attack.

Gem Hammer is a relic in Vampire Crawlers. It can be found in Mad Forest.
## Description
"Unlocks the ability to add Gems to Cards. Find Gems in chests or when levelling up."
## Trivia
- This is one of the three relics to be introduced in the demo of Vampire Crawlers. The two others were the  Combo Stack and the  Guiding Light.
=====
# Crawlers:Gems
Gem Hammer tutorial
Gems in Vampire Crawlers are loot from level ups treasure chests found in dungeons. After the  Gem Hammer relic is acquired, it can be used to apply gems to cards, adding the gem's effect to the card. 
Adding the same gem twice on a card will add the gem's effects twice (e.g. 10% Growth + 10% Growth will give 20% Growth when used). There is currently a bug where the card's description does not update to reflect the 2 gems. 
## Gem rarities
The rarity of a gem depends on its individual rarity and the source. In the case of level ups,  Luck will also play a role.
Gem rarities can be tweaked in the Jeweller menu. However, when Endless Mode is enabled, all gems will drop at their default rarity, regardless if their rarity has been tweaked or if they have been sealed.
## In level ups
Gems have a chance to be awarded in level ups, with Ultra Rare gems being the exception. Uncommon, Rare, and Very Rare gems will appear more frequently with higher Luck, with Luck benefitting the higher rarities more. 
| Rarity | Weight | Luck Factor
| Common | 60 | 0
| Uncommon | 30 | 0.035
| Rare | 20 | 0.065
| Very Rare | 6 | 0.3
| Ultra Rare | 0 | 0
## In treasure chests
Each type of treasure chest has its own set of rarities that can be awarded. Luck does not affect this distribution, unlike level ups. Instead, there is a luck-based chance for chests to award 4 gems instead of 3.
- If the player can evolve a weapon, then the Evolution gem may appear more frequently. Treasure chests are also able to provide multiple Evolution gems within a selection.
In the Jeweller menu, when editing the rarity of Gems, the chests that gem can appear in will be showcased on the right. Chests that are faded out will not contain that gem. When a gem's rarity is increased, it will be removed from its original chest, making it only appear in higher-tier chests. When a gem's rarity is lowered, it will be just be added to lower tier chests, rather than being removed from its original.
| Rarity | Treasure Chest Type(s) Found In | Weight
| Common | Tier 1 | 160
| Uncommon | Tier 1 | 120
| Rare | Tier 1 and Tier 2 | 80
| Very Rare | Tier 2 and Tier 3 | 60
| Ultra Rare | Tier 2 and Tier 3 | 40
- Tier 1 treasure chests are the normal chests found commonly within dungeons. These only reward Common, Uncommon, and Rare gems.
- Tier 2 treasures are the rare, locked, purple chests where the player must sacrifice cards in order to open them. These mostly reward Rare and Very Rare gems with a chance to reward Ultra Rare gems.
- Lastly, tier 3 chests are awarded by defeating bosses (at floor exit / descend, coffins, optional floor boss, and the lone ambush boss). These chests are ornately decorated clad in translucent gold and placed in the middle / center of pathways. These primarily reward Very Rare and Ultra Rare.

This section contains info obtained from datamining and may not currently be available in-game.
"Monster slayer? Sure. Monster business partner? If the price is right. Gennaro Belpaese may share his family’s knack for killing the undead, but he prefers a more… entrepreneurial approach. Armed with twin blades and an even sharper tongue, he’s just as likely to strike a deal as he is to strike a killing blow—whichever pays better."

Giovanna is a character card in Vampire Crawlers. Her character can be unlocked by opening the coffin in Library Sanctum.
## Starter deck
×1
1
Giovanna
[Giovanna]
Add 20% Luck.
Crawler.
×2
1
Gatti Amari
[Gatti Amari]
Deal 40 damage. Chance to Scuffle.
×1
0
Empty Tome
[Empty Tome]
Add 1 Mana.
×1
0
Armor
[Armor]
Add 2 Armor.
## Audio
- "There's only one way, the witch way."
- "As long as I can bring my cats."
- "I don't forget and I don't forgive."
- "Curse them with all of my power."
- Giovanna laughs (evil).
- "I've done plenty."
- "Farewell."
- Giovanna grunts in pain.
- Giovanna grunts in pain and perishes.
- "I'm ready."
- "Hooray."
- "Woe."
- Giovanna groans in disappointment.
- "Lucky you."
- "What are the chances?"
- "Stack the odds."
- "To your aid."
- "Spooky."
- "Shazam."
- "Presto."
- Giovanna laughs.
- "I say."
- "Yes."
- "Smart."
- "Together."
- "We fight."
- "Attack."
- "Engarde."
- "Eureka."
- "Unite."
- "Wild."
- "Free."

Golden Armor is a defense card in Vampire Crawlers. It is the second tier of Armor cards. The minimum level required for this card to appear in level up reward selection is 1.
When sacrificed in a Bing Upgrades vending machine, it will add +2 armor every turn until the end of the run
## Evolution group
The following are cards in the Armor evolution group.
| Card | Cost | Text
| Armor | 0 | Add 2 Armor.
| Golden Armor | 1 | Add 4 Armor.
| Rainbow Armor | 2 | Add 6 Armor.
| Hero's Armor † | 3 | Add 8 Armor.

Gorgeous Moon is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers.
It is evolved from  Pentagram with any card in the  Crown group and an  Evolution gem.

Erase: Remove enemies and gain bonus experience.

# Crawlers:Greed
Greed
This article is about the stat. For the gem, see Crawlers:Greed (gem).
Greed is one of the deck stats in Vampire Crawlers. It is capped at 1,000% (within a dungeon crawl run) and starts at a base of 0.
Greed affects all gold income sources except for the "Cash Out"-options (200 Gold) of the Chests and (Bing Upgrades) Vending Machines.

# Crawlers:Hand
Hand is one of the deck stats in Vampire Crawlers.
It determines how many cards the player draws at the start of each turn, starting with a base of 3.
It does not have a set cap but will reset back to the active base value after each battle.
## PowerUp
| Hand
| Increase Hand by 1 card per rank.
| Dungeon
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| ID | PowerUp_Speed
| Max level | 2
| Bonus per level | +1
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +2
| Unlocked by | Completing Weeny Bridge.
| Cost | 2,500

| {"type":"attack","cost":"2","id":"Card_A_2_HolyWand","unlocked by":"Evolving Magic Wand.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","evofromgroup":"Empty Tome","evofrom":"Magic Wand","image":"","name":"Holy Wand","text":"Deal 160 damage. Prioritize attackers.","release":"21 April 2026","demo":"Yes","max gem slots":"2","gem slots":"1"}
Holy Wand is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers.
It is evolved from  Magic Wand with any card in the  Empty Tome group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Holy Wand has a base  Amount of 10, with each projectile dealing a base Damage of 15. It targets enemies sequentially from right to left prioritizing enemies which are due to attack. Any projectiles in excess of the number of enemies in a row once the leftmost enemy is reached starts again from the right in that same row. Only once there are no enemies remaining in a row will it target the row behind.

## Audio
- "An admirable choice."
- "My magic is not to be feared."
- "May your power know no bounds."
- "One last trick up this wizard's sleeve."

- "Unite."

## Character bio
This section contains info obtained from datamining and may not currently be available in-game.
"Magic is dangerous, they said. It’s untrustworthy, they warned. Imelda heard all that—and promptly ignored it. While her family swings whips like it’s the Middle Ages, she prefers raining arcane destruction from a safe distance. Sure, she’s the black sheep, but when the undead come knocking, guess who suddenly loves a well-placed fireball?"
## Trivia
- There is a statue of her next to the Town Hall.

Inlaid LibraryLibrary West WingLibrary SanctumThe Futile Scribe was cursed to know all and understand nothing. His endless library may gift us insight... or damnation. A mask of stone whispers endlessly within.
Please upload an image!No sane intruder would dare talk louder than a whisper, in this labyrinth of book shelves and cursed tomes. Inhuman forms stalk the halls, seeking long overdue revenge.
Please upload an image!Once home to some of the world's brightest and most talented mages. Now there is nothing but dark magic, while familiars and those who seek to control them.
234Crawling, Turbo? (edit)? (edit)
Inlaid Library is a dungeon in Vampire Crawlers. It is unlocked by reaching level 10 in Mad Forest, and contains 3 difficulties, named Inlaid Library, Library West Wing, and Library Sanctum.
Reaching level 15 in Inlaid Library will unlock Teeny Bridge.
## Inlaid Library
## Enemies
Enemies appearing in Inlaid Library:
-  Mudman
-  MudmanElite - boss of floor 1
-  Ecto
-  GlowingBat Elite - floor 2 optional chest miniboss
-  EctoElite - boss of floor 2
-  Ghost
-  GhostElite - boss of floor 3
-  LionHead
-  ShadeBomb - during floor 4 miniboss encounter
-  NesuferitElite - floor 4 optional chest miniboxx
-  LionHeadElite - boss of floor 4
-  SneakyHead
-  QueenMedusaElite - boss of floor 5
## Events
Dungeon events appearing in Inlaid Library:
| Floor | Spawn count | Total weight | Event | Weight | Probability | Is guaranteed
| 1 | 3 | 0 | Gem chest | 1 | Infinity | [Green checkmark]
| 0 | Random event
| 1 | Evolution statue | 1 | 1 | [Red X]
| 2 | 2 | 0 | Random event
| 0 | Random event

## Unlocks
The following relics and characters can be unlocked from each of these dungeons listed below:
- Inlaid Library:  Guiding Light
- Library West Wing:  Grim Grimoire
- Library Sanctum:  Giovanna,  Arcana Finder
## Trivia
- In Inlaid Library, a rearranged version of Reincarnated Echoes is played.
=====
# Crawlers:Inn
| Inn
| The Gorton Bell Inn. Relax with a pint of milk and ask mighty Crawlers to join your party.
| Building
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Function | Allows for buying and equipping Crawlers.
| Unlocking | Completing Floor 1 in Mad Forest.
| Music | Never Too Prepared (Crawlers Inn)/Never Too Prepared (Crawlers Inn - Disco)
| Chronology
| ← Previous | Current | Next →
| Settings | Inn | Fortune Teller
| {"description":"The Gorton Bell Inn. Relax with a pint of milk and ask mighty Crawlers to join your party.","next":"Fortune Teller","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","function":"Allows for buying and equipping Crawlers.","image":"","name":"Inn","prev":"Settings","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Never Too Prepared (Crawlers Inn)/Never Too Prepared (Crawlers Inn - Disco)","unlocking":"Completing Floor 1 in Mad Forest."}
The Gorton Bell Inn, or simply the Inn, is one of the eight buildings that can be found in the Village. It allows for the player to buy and equip Crawlers.
It is unlocked by completing Floor 1 in Mad Forest. Doing so would also unlock the ability to purchase Imelda.
## List of characters
See Crawlers:Crawlers
## Disco mode
TBA
## Trivia
- This building is named after "Gorton", which is the name of the Village that it is located in.
- It is the only building to have more than one music track: the normal one and the disco version.
- The milk that is consumed in this building might be sourced from the Dairy Plant.
=====

King Bible

| In demo | Yes

| {"type":"attack","cost":"1","id":"Card_A_1_KingBible","text":"Deal 40 damage with a chance to Knockback.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","evointo":"Unholy Vespers","image":"","name":"King Bible","demo":"Yes","release":"21 April 2026","max gem slots":"2","gem slots":"1","evointogroup":"Spellbinder"}
King Bible is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers. It starts with 1 gem slot. The minimum level required for this card to appear in level up reward selection is 1.
It can be evolved into  Unholy Vespers with any card in the  Spellbinder group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
King Bible has a base  Amount of 5, with each projectile dealing a base of Damage of 8. It targets enemies in the front row sequentially from left to right. Any projectiles in excess of the number of enemies in a row once the rightmost enemy is reached starts again from the left in that same row. It will not target enemies not in the front row, even enemies with no enemy in front of them.
=====

| {"type":"attack","cost":"0","id":"Card_A_0_Knife","text":"Deal 40 damage.Crit.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","evointo":"Thousand Edge","image":"","name":"Knife","demo":"Yes","release":"21 April 2026","max gem slots":"2","gem slots":"1","evointogroup":"Bracer"}
Knife is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers. It starts with 1 gem slot. The minimum level required for this card to appear in level up reward selection is 1.
It can be evolved into  Thousand Edge with any card in the  Bracer group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Knife has a base  Amount of 5, with each projectile dealing a base Damage of 8. It targets enemies sequentially from right to left, starting from the front row. Any projectiles in excess of the number of enemies in a row once the leftmost enemy is reached starts again from the right in that same row. Only once there are no enemies remaining in a row will it target the row behind.
Its special effect, Crit, allows for a chance for the player to deal multiplied damage to enemies.
## Trivia
- It is the first card to be used by a player in the game. It is first used at the beginning of the Tutorial.

## Audio
- "I am not worried about intimidating men."

## Character bio
This section contains info obtained from datamining and may not currently be available in-game.
"Raised by Porta, feared by everyone else, Lama Ladonna doesn’t just win battles—she ends them. A warrior of legendary skill and terrifying presence, her mere arrival on the battlefield has been known to make enemies lose their nerve… and their dignity. Those lucky enough to lack bowels might stand a chance. The rest? Well, they’d better hope she’s in a good mood."

A vampire is said to be the root of this evil, but we can find only mayhem and roast chicken.","name3":"Berserk Wood","name1":"Mad Forest","image3":"","version3":"Berserk Wood","image2":"","floors3":"5","version1":"Mad Forest","difficulty2":"2","floors2":"5","floors1":"4","difficulty3":"3","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Crawling, Turbo","difficulty1":"1","description2":"The deeper the forest goes the more unsettling the monsters become. Mad ForestFurious ForestBerserk WoodOnce a thriving haven, now a dumping ground for evil. A vampire is said to be the root of this evil, but we can find only mayhem and roast chicken.
The deeper the forest goes the more unsettling the monsters become. Something in the air must be pushing them to insanity. Dark magic or maybe the quality of the chicken.
There is no turning back from the depths once inside. A muffled cry can be heard between the trees, along with flapping of a thousand wings.
123455
Mad Forest is a dungeon in Vampire Crawlers. It is unlocked with the World Map. It contains 3 difficulties, named Mad Forest, Furious Forest, and Berserk Wood.
Reaching level 10 in Mad Forest will unlock Inlaid Library.
## Mad Forest
## Enemies
Enemies appearing in Mad Forest:
-  Bat
-  Bat Elite - boss of floor 1
-  Skeleton3
-  Skeleton3 Elite - boss of floor 2
-  Ghoul
-  TreasureBat Elite - floor 3 optional chest miniboss
-  Zombie Elite - boss of floor 3
-  FlowerWall - during floor 4 boss encounter
-  MantisElite - boss of floor 4
## Events
Dungeon events appearing in Mad Forest:
| Floor | Spawn count | Total weight | Event | Weight | Probability | Is guaranteed
| 1 | 1 | 1 | Evolution statue | 1 | 1 | [Red X]
| 2 | 1 | 6 | Mana statue | 5 | 0.8333333 | [Red X]
| Arcana choice | 1 | 0.16666667 | [Red X]
| 3 | 1 | 0 | Crystal | 1 | Infinity | [Green checkmark]
| 4 | 1 | 0 | Gem chest | 1 | Infinity | [Green checkmark]
## Unlocks
The following relics and characters can be unlocked from each of these dungeons listed below:
- Mad Forest:  Gem Hammer
- Furious Forest:  Polentír
- Berserk Wood:  Pugnala
A new player can just clear the first floor to unlock the Inn, which would then unlock the ability to buy Imelda.
## Trivia
- In Mad Forest, a rearranged version of Forest Night Fever is played.
=====

| {"type":"attack","cost":"0","id":"Card_A_1_MagicWand","text":"Deal 30 damage. Prioritize attackers.","unlock":"Available by default.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","evointo":"Holy Wand","image":"","name":"Magic Wand","max gem slots":"2","release":"21 April 2026","gem slots":"1","demo":"Yes","evointogroup":"Empty Tome"}
Magic Wand is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers. It is present in  Imelda's starting deck. It starts with 1 gem slot and is unlocked by default. The minimum level required for this card to appear in level up reward selection is 1.
It can be evolved into  Holy Wand with any card in the  Empty Tome group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Magic Wand has a base  Amount of 1, with each projectile dealing a base Damage of 30. It targets enemies sequentially from right to left prioritizing enemies which are due to attack. Any projectiles in excess of the number of enemies in a row once the leftmost enemy is reached starts again from the right in that same row. Only once there are no enemies remaining in a row will it target the row behind.
=====
# Crawlers:Magnet
Magnet
Magnet is one of the deck stats in Vampire Crawlers.
It is capped at 4 (or at 9 when MissingN0 is the leading main Crawler) and starts at a base of 0.
It boosts the effect of draw cards such as the  Attractorb. For example, a Magnet stat of 1 would make the Attractorb card draw 2 cards.

| Mild
| 
| Gem
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| In demo | No
| Rarity | Ultra Rare
| Card text | Never leaves the Hand, but always breaks Combo.
| Unlocked by | Completing Gallo Tower with Gallo.
| {"unlocked by":"Completing Gallo Tower with Gallo.","image":"","name":"Mild","rarity":"Ultra Rare","release":"21 April 2026","text":"Never leaves the Hand, but always breaks Combo.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","demo":"No"}
Mild is a gem in Vampire Crawlers.
## Anti-synergies
- If a card has both the Mild gem and the  Combo Breaker gem, the Mild gem's effect takes precedence and would still break the combo upon usage.
## Tips
- Using it on a card with no Mana cost will help with not spending Mana. Using it on a card with a negative Mana cost will help gain additional Mana.
- However, in both cases, the card may eventually shattered if used too many times.

# Crawlers:Milky Way Map
Milky Way Map
| Milky Way Map
| Shows enemy positions and more on the mini map.
| Relic
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| ID | RelicConfig_MilkyWayMap
| Effects | Makes enemy demarcations (skull) visible on the minimap.
| Found in | Available from start.
| {"foundin":"Available from start.","image":"","description":"Shows enemy positions and more on the mini map.","name":"Milky Way Map","release":"21 April 2026","id":"RelicConfig_MilkyWayMap","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","effects":"Makes enemy demarcations (skull) visible on the minimap."}
Milky Way Map is a relic in Vampire Crawlers. It makes enemy demarcations (skull) visible on the minimap.
This relic is available from the very start of a save file, including the Tutorial. Though, unlocking the Museum would allow for disabling or enabling this relic.
## Description
"Shows enemy positions and more on the mini map."
## Trivia
- This is one of the two relics that are unlocked at the very start of the game, along with the  Rilevatore. This also means that it has no dedicated unlock or obtaining animation/screen, since it is already unlocked in the first place.
- The  Combo Stack is also available starting from the Tutorial, but unlike those two aforementioned relics, this relic is unlocked in the midst of the Tutorial, rather than from the very start.
=====
# Crawlers:MiniMap
Example map layout.
The MiniMap is part of the interface in Vampire Crawlers. It displays the layout of the current dungeon floor along with all the enemies, treasure chests, and other events. After obtaining the  Guiding Light relic, it also displays the location of breakable light sources.
=====

MissingN0 is a character card in Vampire Crawlers. The card is unlocked by defeating Red Death outside the demo version of the game.
MissingN0's base stats are much higher than any other crawler. It is also the only crawler to start with an Evolved weapon. Whether or not this is intentional, MissingN0's Death Spirals do not come with an Evolution Gem.
When picked or recruited as a follower (not as the leading main crawler), MissingN0 adds a singular Whip rather than any cards from the starting deck selection.

The following clips are reversed versions of  Gennaro's voice lines with added effects:

- MissingN0's stats are directly based off the highest possible stats MissingN▯ can roll in Vampire Survivors.

# Crawlers:Mug
| Mug
| 
| Gem
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| In demo | No
| Rarity | Common
| Card text | Earns coins when enemy dies.
| Unlocked by | Collecting 3,333 coins.
| {"unlocked by":"Collecting 3,333 coins.","image":"","name":"Mug","rarity":"Common","release":"21 April 2026","text":"Earns coins when enemy dies.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","demo":"No"}
Mug is a gem in Vampire Crawlers.
## Effects
- The Mug Gem in essence Vicious Hunger in a gem form.
- Using a weapon with this gem will tag all enemies hit with a status effect. Upon death, the player is rewarded 3 coins which scale with the Greed.
- It's worth noting that even if the enemies don't die from the weapon equipped with this gem, affected enemies will still reward coins when slain from other weapons.
- A visual effect to reflect affected enemies are show as coins floating upwards in front of their sprite
=====
# Crawlers:Museum
| Museum
| The Museum of antiquities. Here you can peruse a collection of Relics.
| Building
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Function | Listing unlocked Relics and allowing to enable or disable certain relics.
| Unlocking | Playing two Dungeons.
| Music | Never Too Prepared (Relics)
| Chronology
| ← Previous | Current | Next →
| Blacksmith | Museum | Shop
| {"description":"The Museum of antiquities. Here you can peruse a collection of Relics.","next":"Shop","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","function":"Listing unlocked Relics and allowing to enable or disable certain relics.","image":"","name":"Museum","prev":"Blacksmith","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Never Too Prepared (Relics)","unlocking":"Playing two Dungeons."}
The Relic Museum, or simply the Museum, is one of the eight buildings that can be found in the Village. Its purpose is to list unlocked Relics, as well as to allow disabling and/or (re-)enabling certain relics. Relics that are not unlocked yet would remain grayed out.
It is unlocked after playing in two Dungeons.
## List of relics
Main article: Crawlers:Relics
The following table indicates the row and column as to where each relic is placed in the Museum.
| Relic placements in Museum
| Stardust Anvil | Bomba Infernale | Gem Hammer | Grim Grimoire | Guiding Light
| Lapidary Loupe | Milky Way Map | Ovenkilt | Overkill | Combo Stack
| Polentír | Arcana Finder | Rilevatore | Sorceress' Tears | Ultimate Ultra Overkill
| Seventh Trumpet
## Trivia
- Upon unlocking this building, the Museum would show that the player has the  Milky Way Map and the  Rilevatore unlocked and activated. This is the case for both of these relics, which have been unlocked and activated since the start of a save file, including the Tutorial.
- Technically speaking, it can be said that the Museum unlocks the ability to disable (and/or (re-)enable) these relics.
- Just outside the Museum entrance, there are four statues of Dommario.
- This is one of the three buildings to be introduced in the 1.0 release of the game. The other two are the Blacksmith and the Fortune Teller.
=====
# Crawlers:Music
Music
Cover art
Music in Vampire Crawlers consists primarily of background tracks played during dungeon runs.
## List of music
## Menus
Most menu music is named "Never Too Prepared" with the Town theme being the original version, and all others being different arrangements of it. Other songs used in menus are for the main menu and the treasure chest opening music.
| Menu | Music track
| Arcana | Never Too Prepared (Arcana)
| Blacksmith | Never Too Prepared (Blacksmith)
| Inn | Never Too Prepared (Crawlers Inn)
| Inn (Disco) | Never Too Prepared (Crawlers Inn - Disco)
| Main Menu | Il Cuor non si Spaura
| Power Ups | Never Too Prepared (Power Ups)
| Relics | Never Too Prepared (Relics)
| Town | Never Too Prepared
| Treasure | Find Treasures
| World Map | Never Too Prepared (World Map)
## Dungeons
Each dungeon has an ambient "crawling" version and a "turbo" arrangement used in combat.
| Dungeon | Music track (Crawling) | Turbo
| Tutorial | Prelude of Fight | Turbo
| Mad Forest | Forest Night Fever | Turbo
| Inlaid Library | Reincarnated Echoes | Turbo
| Bridge | Roaring Thunder | Turbo
| Dairy Plant | It's Raining Minotaurs | Turbo
| Gallo Tower | Gaze Up at the Stars | Turbo
| Cappella Magna | I'm Every Reaper | Turbo
## Treasures
Each treasure chest has a jingle on opening.
| Treasure | Music track
| Treasure Chest | Treasure A
| Golden Chest | Treasure B
| Arcana Chest | Treasure C
| Locked Chest | Treasure D
## Enemies
Some boss enemies have their own unique music tracks.
| Enemy | Music track
| Trickster | Shinigami Tensei
| Final Boss Phase 1 | Ozmic Theatre
| Final Boss Phase 2 | Clutching the Curtains
| Final Boss Phase 3 | Plot Overkill

# Crawlers:Never Too Prepared (disambiguation)
Never Too Prepared may refer to the following music tracks:
- Crawlers:Never Too Prepared, played while in Village, Town Hall, or Graveyard.
- Crawlers:Never Too Prepared (Arcana), played while in Fortune Teller.
- Crawlers:Never Too Prepared (Blacksmith), played while in Blacksmith.
- Crawlers:Never Too Prepared (Crawlers Inn), played while in Inn and not in disco mode.
- Crawlers:Never Too Prepared (Crawlers Inn - Disco), played while in Inn and in disco mode.
- Crawlers:Never Too Prepared (Power Ups), played while in Shop.

# Crawlers:Outhouse
Outhouse is a dungeon event in Vampire Crawlers. 
As the title suggest, this is but a simple medieval fantasy outhouse. Or in modern a non-fantasy term, a toilet (but not a water closet (WC), or could it be...).
Interacting with Outhouse (by "knocking" the door when walking into) allows recruiting a random unlocked / purchased Crawler for a variable amount of gold (generally between 100 and 500) for the rest of the duration of the dungeon crawl run / session.
There is no upper limit / cap of how many crawlers one can have active during a dungeon crawl.
=====

Overkill is a relic in Vampire Crawlers. It can be found in Teeny Bridge. Dealing extra damage to bosses after death can earn extra coins. Every 10 damage earns 1 coin up to 5000 coins. The damage that kills the boss doesn't count.

| {"type":"attack","cost":"3","id":"Card_A_5_Peachone","unlocked by":"Reaching level 25.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","demo":"Yes","evointo":"Vandalier","image":"","name":"Peachone","text":"Deal 100 damage. Bombards enemies after every turn.","release":"21 April 2026","max gem slots":"3","gem slots":"2","evointogroup":"Ebony Wings"}
Peachone is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers. The minimum level required for this card to appear in level up reward selection is 20.
It can be evolved into  Vandalier with  Ebony Wings and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Peachone has a base  Amount of 5, with each projectile (e.g. each dove) dealing five separate instances of base Damage of 20.
Each instance targets enemies sequentially from right to left, starting from the front row. Any instances in excess of the number of enemies in a row once the leftmost enemy is reached starts again from the right in that same row. Only once there are no enemies remaining in a row will an instance target the row behind.
=====

## Character bio
This section contains info obtained from datamining and may not currently be available in-game.
"Poe has few friends not because he’s lacking in charm — though he is — but because his all-garlic diet makes him unbearable to be around. This has made him an unlikely, but highly efficient monster-hunter, with the ability to clear rooms faster than a warewolf at full moon. His pitiful, lonely life has led him to reflect on an inner strength others overlook. His defenses may seem weak, but they’re his greatest power, drawn from solitude to face any threat."
## Trivia
- The retired version of Poe can be found in front of the Town Hall.

## Character bio
This section contains info obtained from datamining and may not currently be available in-game.
"From pigsty to power, Porta Ladonna never lets opportunity—or a foolish noble—go to waste. Clever, charming, and utterly ruthless, she used one romantic tryst to secure wealth, then promptly ditched the count to carve out her own empire. Some say she’s a gold-digger, but Porta prefers the term self-made woman—and she’s not carrying anyone who can’t keep up."
=====
# Crawlers:Power Ups
PowerUps
Power Ups is a menu in Vampire Crawlers where players can purchase permanent boosts for their deck stats. The Power Ups Shop is unlocked by obtaining 250 coins.
Tier 2 of the shop is unlocked after completing Teeny Bridge, and tier 3 is unlocked after completing Weeny Bridge.
## Power Ups list
| Sprite | Name | Description | Max level | Bonus per level | Max effect | Unlocked by | Cost
| | Amount | Weapons fire 1 more projectile per rank. | 3 | +1 | +3 | Completing Teeny Bridge. | 1,250
| | Area | Attacks deal splash damage. 10% per rank. | 5 | +10% | +50% | Accessing Power Ups shop | 850
| | Armor | Add 1 Armor per rank. | 3 | +1 | +3 | Accessing Power Ups shop | 1,250
| | Banish | Remove 2 cards from level up choices per rank. | 5 | +2 | +10 | Completing Weeny Bridge. | 300
| | Crawler Slot | Add an extra Crawler slot per rank. | 2 | +1 | +2 | Completing Teeny Bridge. | 3,500
| | Curse | Increase enemy strength by 20% per rank, but gain additional XP. | 5 | +20% | +100% | Completing Weeny Bridge. | 350
| | Duration | Increase Duration Crawlers stay by 1 per rank. | 5 | +1 | +5 | Accessing Power Ups shop | 250
| | Greed | Gain 25% more coins per rank. | 4 | +25% | +100% | Completing Inlaid Library. | 1,000
| | Growth | Gain 20% more XP per rank. | 5 | +20% | +100% | Accessing Power Ups shop | 750
| | Hand | Increase Hand by 1 card per rank. | 2 | +1 | +2 | Completing Weeny Bridge. | 2,500
| | Luck | Increase Luck by 20% per rank. | 5 | +20% | +100% | Accessing Power Ups shop | 400
| | Magnet | Increase Draw effects by 1 card per rank. | 1 | +1 | +1 | Completing Weeny Bridge. | 2,000
| | Cooldown | Add 1 Mana per rank. | 2 | +1 | +2 | Completing Teeny Bridge. | 3,500
| | Max Health | Increase Max Health by 10% per rank. | 5 | +10% | +50% | Accessing Power Ups shop | 450
| | Might | Deal 20% more damage per rank. | 5 | +20% | +100% | Accessing Power Ups shop | 500
| | Recovery | Heal 1 HP per rank after each encounter | 3 | +1 | +3 | Accessing Power Ups shop | 500
| | Reroll | Reroll choices, 2 per rank. | 5 | +2 | +10 | Accessing Power Ups shop | 200

# Crawlers:RedDeath
The Reaper
| RedDeath
| 
| Enemy
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Base health | 1,000,000
| Level scale mult | 0.01
| Experience | 0
| Knockback resist | 99
| Disarm resist | 95
| Difficulty | 0
| Instakill resist | 1
| Boss | No
| Chest tiers | 0
| Max hit | 333
| Max armor | 0
| Wound chance | 0
| Total chance | 300
| Enemy types | RedReaper
| Appears in | All
| {"chesttier":"0","woundchance":"0","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","disarmresist":"95","knockbackresist":"99","totalchance":"300","types":"RedReaper","levelscale":"0.01","instakillresist":"1","xp":"0","dungeons":"All","difficulty":"0","maxarmor":"0","name":"RedDeath","maxhit":"333","release":"21 April 2026","hp":"1000000","image":"","boss":"No"}
RedDeath is an enemy in Vampire Crawlers. It is encountered at the end of the final floor in every dungeon, and attacks first before the player draws cards. Typically this will kill the player and end the run as a successful completion of the dungeon, however, if enough max health has been obtained, this initial attack can be survived. Despite being a difficult enemy to defeat and having similar mechanics to other bosses, internally it is neither classified as a boss nor has a high difficulty value. Interacting with the final shovel again after killing a RedDeath summons another RedDeath.
In the demo, defeating RedDeath does not have any benefit or unlock.
In the full game, defeating RedDeath unlocks the MissingN0 crawler, functioning similar to the Mask of the Red Death character in Vampire Survivors. Each RedDeath defeated also adds +100% temporary Curse, increasing the max hit and health of subsequent RedDeaths.
=====

# Crawlers:Relics
Relics
Relics in Vampire Crawlers are unique treasure found hidden in various dungeons. They unlock new mechanics in the game.
Although the concept of relics was introduced in the demo, with the inclusion of the  Combo Stack, the  Gem Hammer, and the  Guiding Light, the ability to browse all collected relics in the Museum was introduced in the full game's release.
## List of relics
| Sprite | Name | Description | Effects | Found in
| | Arcana Finder | "Adds Arcana chests to dungeons." | Unlocks chests containing Arcanas in dungeons. | Library Sanctum
| | Bomba Infernale | "Instantly vaporise all enemies on the first floor of a dungeon you have previously cleared." | Adds a button that kills all Enemies on the first floor of a previously completed Dungeon and collects their XP. | Dairy Plant
| | Combo Stack | "Play cards in ascending Mana cost order for a Combo Multiplier." | Allows creating combos by playing cards based on the ascending order of Mana costs. | Tutorial
| | Deck Box | "A simple box to hold a deck of cards. Nobody should travel outside of the Village without one of these!" | | 
| | Gem Hammer | "Unlocks the ability to add Gems to Cards. Find Gems in chests or when levelling up." | Allows applying Gems on Cards with empty gem slots. | Mad Forest
| | Grim Grimoire | "Adds a list of discovered weapon evolutions and unions to the Pause menu." | Unlocks "Grim Grimoire" button in the pause menu, which lists all unlocked Evolutions. | Library West Wing
| | Guiding Light | "Shows breakable light sources on the minimap." | Makes light source demarcations (usually little dots) visible on the minimap. | Inlaid Library
| | Lapidary Loupe | "Unlocks the Jeweller's counter at the Blacksmith." | Unlocks the Jeweller, which catalogs and allows managing Gems. | Gallo Tower
| | Milky Way Map | "Shows enemy positions and more on the mini map." | Makes enemy demarcations (skull) visible on the minimap. | Available from start.
| | Ovenkilt | "Overkill in any encounter." | | Cappella Ultima
| | Overkill | "Keep attacking defeated bosses to earn coins." | Allows dealing extra damage to bosses to earn extra coins. | Teeny Bridge
| | Polentír | "Unlocks the Fortune Teller's Arcana tent in the Village." | Unlocks the ability to choose an unlocked Arcana before entering a dungeon. | Furious Forest
| | Rilevatore | "Show how weapons will affect targets. Plus, you look hella cool wearing them. Instant rizz!" | Shows weapon effects on target enemies. | Available from start.
| | Seventh Trumpet | "Adds an Endless button to the World Map." | Unlocks Endless Mode. | ???
| | Sorceress' Tears | "Adds a Hurry button to the World Map." | Unlocks Hurry Mode. | Gallo Tower
| | Stardust Anvil | "Unlock the Blacksmith's Workshop in the Village." | Unlocks the Blacksmith, where more gem slots can be added on specific cards. | Teeny Bridge
| | Ultimate Ultra Overkill | "Limit breaks Overkill." | Allows dealing extra damage to bosses to earn more extra coins. | Curd Refinery

# Crawlers:Return
| Return
| 
| Gem
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| In demo | Yes
| Rarity | Rare
| Card text | Returns to hand after playing.
| Unlocked by | Playing 300 cards.
| {"unlocked by":"Playing 300 cards.","image":"","name":"Return","rarity":"Rare","release":"21 April 2026","text":"Returns to hand after playing.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","demo":"Yes"}
Return is a gem in Vampire Crawlers.
Once per turn, the card slotted with Return gem technically does not leave the hand once used. Since the card has not left the hand, the current on-going combo remains unchanged. Re-using the same card with an exhausted Return gem plays the card normally and then ending into the combo-discard-pile.
## Tips
- Players can use a card with this gem once, then use another card with the same mana cost to continue with the combo, instead of having necessarily to use a card with the Return gem twice.
## Synergies
- Pairing this gem with  Reduce Mana Cost (-1) or  Reduce Mana Cost (-2) (or for any card with negative mana cost) will double the mana gained to the player.
=====

# Crawlers:Rilevatore
| Rilevatore
| Show how weapons will affect targets. Plus, you look hella cool wearing them. Instant rizz!
| Relic
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| ID | RelicConfig_DamageIndicators
| Effects | Shows weapon effects on target enemies.
| Found in | Available from start.
| {"foundin":"Available from start.","image":"","description":"Show how weapons will affect targets. Plus, you look hella cool wearing them. Instant rizz!","name":"Rilevatore","release":"21 April 2026","id":"RelicConfig_DamageIndicators","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","effects":"Shows weapon effects on target enemies."}
Rilevatore is a relic in Vampire Crawlers. It allows for showing weapon effects on target enemies, such as the damage that will be dealt by an Attack Card.
This relic is available from the very start of a save file, including the Tutorial. Though, unlocking the Museum would allow for disabling or enabling this relic.
## Description
"Show how weapons will affect targets. Plus, you look hella cool wearing them. Instant rizz!"
## Trivia
- This is one of the two relics that are unlocked at the very start of the game, along with the  Milky Way Map. This also means that it has no dedicated unlock or obtaining animation/screen, since it is already unlocked in the first place.
- The  Combo Stack is also available starting from the Tutorial, but unlike those two aforementioned relics, this relic is unlocked in the midst of the Tutorial, rather than from the very start.
=====
