# Crawlers:Settings
Options
| Settings
| A Graveyard seems the most appropriate place for an Options menu.
| Building
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Function | Lists game settings.
| Unlocking | Completing Tutorial.
| Music | Never Too Prepared
| Chronology
| ← Previous | Current | Next →
| Town Hall | Settings | Inn
| {"description":"A Graveyard seems the most appropriate place for an Options menu.","next":"Inn","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","function":"Lists game settings.","image":"","name":"Settings","prev":"Town Hall","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Never Too Prepared","unlocking":"Completing Tutorial.'\"`UNIQ--ref-00000000-QINU`\"'"}
Settings, Options, or the Graveyard, is one of the eight buildings that can be found in the Village and an interface.
Settings can be accessed in multiple ways. Any of the following options can work:
- Clicking the "Pause" button;
- From its corresponding building in the Village;
- Pressing Esc while in a dungeon, the World Map, or the Village (but not inside any other building).
## List of settings
There are six sections of options: Gameplay, Graphics Settings, Sound and Music, Accessibility, Keyboard Input, and Controller Input (controller). In the bottom-right corner it also contains a detailed game version number.
## Gameplay
The Gameplay section contains all the options affecting how mechanics are displayed or used.
- Instant Movement
- Invert Pan
- Rotate Map With Player
- Power Saving Mode
- Auto End Turn
- 3D Card View
- Card Reordering Delay
- Language
- English
- French (Français)
- German (Deutsch)
- Japanese (日本語)
- Italian (Italiano)
- Spanish (Español)
- Traditional Chinese (繁體中文)
- Simplified Chinese (简体中文)
- Brazilian Protuguese (Português (Brasil))
- Korean (한국인)
- Polish (Polski)
- Russian (Русский)
## Graphics Settings
The Graphics Settings section contains options related to how the game is displayed.
- Resolution
- Refresh Rate
- Display Mode
- V-sync
- Motion Blur
- Bloom
## Sound and Music
The Sound and Music section contains volume sliders for various types of sounds.
- Master Volume
- Music Volume
- SFX Volume
- Dialogue Volume
## Accessibility
The Accessibility section contains various options to reduce discomfort.
- Reduce Input Holds
- Screen Shake
- Enemy Shake
- Reduce Flashing
- Grid View
- Dungeon UI Layout
- Layout 1
- Layout 2
## Keyboard Input
The Keyboard Input section lists all the keyboard controls and allows rebinding any of them.
| Action | Binding
| Move Forward | W
| Move Backwards | S
| Move Left | A
| Move Right | D
| Move Forward | Up Arrow
| Move Backwards | Down Arrow
| Turn Left | Left Arrow
| Turn Right | Right Arrow
| Turn Left | Q
| Turn Right | E
| Show Deck Pile | Z
| Show Discard Pile | C
| Play All | G
| End Turn | H
| Toggle Tooltips | T
## Controller Input
The Controller Input section lists all the controller controls, with a few selectable presets.
| Action | Layout 1 | Layout 2 | Layout 3
| Move | Left Stick | Left Stick | Left Stick
| Strafe | Left Stick | Right Stick | Left Stick
| Turn | Right Stick | Left Stick | Right Stick
| Interact | A | A | A
| Play All | X | X | B
| Toggle Tooltips | Y | Y | Y
| Move | D-pad | D-pad | D-pad
| Strafe | D-pad | LT/RT | D-pad
| Turn | LT/RT | D-pad | LT/RT
| Cancel | B | B | B
| End Turn | B | B | X
- Increase Drag Detection Time
## Trivia
- This is one of the two buildings in the Village where the Village's exact track, "Never Too Prepared", continues to play. The other building, where that is also the case, is the Town Hall.
- When the player presses the "Pause" button while either in the Village or the World Map, the game will show an animation where the player is being led to the Graveyard first before accessing the settings menu.
- This is one of the three areas, within the Village, that are visible after completing the Tutorial, with the other being the Town Hall and the World Map.
=====

# Crawlers:Shop
| Shop
| Purchase Power Ups to boost your stats. Got some rare things on sale, stranger!
| Building
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Function | Allows for purchasing and upgrading Power Ups.
| Unlocking | Collecting 250 coins.
| Music | Never Too Prepared (Power Ups)
| Chronology
| ← Previous | Current | Next →
| Museum | Shop | Town Hall
| {"description":"Purchase Power Ups to boost your stats. Got some rare things on sale, stranger!","next":"Town Hall","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","function":"Allows for purchasing and upgrading Power Ups.","image":"","name":"Shop","prev":"Museum","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Never Too Prepared (Power Ups)","unlocking":"Collecting 250 coins."}
The Power Ups Shop, or simply the Shop, is one of the eight buildings that can be found in the Village. Its purpose is to allow for the player to purchase and upgrade Power Ups.
It is unlocked after the player collects a total of 250 coins.
## List of power ups
See Crawlers:Power Ups
## Trivia
- By default, the game will show this building upon booting their save file. This is also the case if they have quit, lost, or won in a dungeon.
- However, if the player did not unlock the Shop, the default building that would show up would be the Town Hall instead.
=====

| {"type":"attack","cost":"3","id":"Card_A_1_SoulEater","unlocked by":"Evolving Garlic.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","evofromgroup":"Pummarola","evofrom":"Garlic","image":"","name":"Soul Eater","text":"Deal 20 damage to the front row. Heal 2. Disarm*.","release":"21 April 2026","max gem slots":"2","gem slots":"1","demo":"Yes"}
Soul Eater is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers.
It is evolved from  Garlic with any card in the  Pummarola group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Soul Eater has a base  Amount of 1, with each projectile dealing a base Damage of 100 to every enemy in the front row. It will not target enemies not in the front row, even enemies with no enemy in front of them.
It does not benefit from any bonuses to Amount.
Disarm* : High chance of preventing 2 enemy actions.
=====

Tiny Bridge
| Teeny Bridge
| This handsome little bridge seems perfectly safe to cross. Definitely better than swimming, anyway.
| Dungeon
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Difficulty | 3
| Floors | 1

| {"description":"This handsome little bridge seems perfectly safe to cross. Definitely better than swimming, anyway.","order":"4","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","difficulty":"3","image":"","name":"Teeny Bridge","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Crawling, Turbo","floors":"1"}
Teeny Bridge is a dungeon in Vampire Crawlers. It is unlocked by reaching level 15 in Inlaid Library.
Completing this dungeon will unlock Dairy Plant.
## Unlocks
The following relics can be unlocked in Teeny Bridge:
-  Stardust Anvil
-  Overkill
## Trivia
- It is the only dungeon, that is originally from Vampire Survivors, that does not share the exact same name as its Vampire Survivors counterpart. The difference is in the word "Teeny"/"Tiny".
- It is the only bridge dungeon that has unlockable relics.
=====

| {"type":"attack","cost":"3","id":"Card_A_3_ThousandEdge","unlocked by":"Evolving Knife.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","evofromgroup":"Bracer","evofrom":"Knife","image":"","name":"Thousand Edge","text":"Deal 200 damage.Crit.","release":"21 April 2026","max gem slots":"2","gem slots":"1","demo":"Yes"}
Thousand Edge is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers.
It is evolved from  Knife with any card in the  Bracer group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Thousand Edge has a base  Amount of 20, with each projectile dealing a base Damage of 9. It targets enemies sequentially from right to left, starting from the front row. Any projectiles in excess of the number of enemies in a row once the leftmost enemy is reached starts again from the right in that same row. Only once there are no enemies remaining in a row will it target the row behind.

# Crawlers:Town Hall
| Town Hall
| Track your Unlocks.
| Building
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Function | Lists Unlocks.
| Unlocking | Completing Tutorial.
| Music | Never Too Prepared
| Chronology
| ← Previous | Current | Next →
| Shop | Town Hall | Settings
| {"description":"Track your Unlocks.","next":"Settings","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","function":"Lists Unlocks.","image":"","name":"Town Hall","prev":"Shop","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Never Too Prepared","unlocking":"Completing Tutorial."}
The Town Hall is one of the eight buildings that can be found in the Village. Its purpose is to list and track Unlocks.
It becomes available after the player completes the Tutorial.
## List of unlocks
See Crawlers:Unlocks
## Trivia
- Just outside of the Town Hall, the retired version of Poe can be found.
- To the far right, there is also a statue of Imelda.
- This is one of the two buildings in the Village where the Village's exact track, "Never Too Prepared", continues to play. The other building, where that is also the case, is the Graveyard.
- If the player does not have the Shop unlocked in a save file, by default, the game will show this building upon booting their save file. This is also the case if they have quit, lost, or won in a dungeon.
- This is one of the three areas, within the Village, that are visible after completing the Tutorial, with the other being the Graveyard and the World Map.

# Crawlers:Treasure chest
Treasure Chest
Treasure chest is a dungeon event in Vampire Crawlers. Treasure chests contain gems and may be scattered throughout the dungeon or rewarded from Elite and Boss encounters.
There are 3 types of treasure chests which reward gems in the dungeon:
- Tier 1 Chests are red and gold, and may reward Common to Rare gems.
- Tier 2 Chests are purple and have two locks on them and may contain Rare to Ultra-Rare gems.
- Tier 3 Chests are gold with many decorations on them and may contain Very Rare to Ultra-Rare gems.
For more information, see the Gems page
=====

Trickster is an enemy in Vampire Crawlers. Despite being a difficult enemy to defeat, internally it is neither classified as a boss nor has a high difficulty value. In the demo, there is no reward or unlock for defeating the Trickster. In the full game, defeating it unlocks the  Uncrackable gem.
It will spawn during any encounter when the same card is played five times in a single turn. When the card is played for the fifth time, it shatters, summoning the Trickster.

# Crawlers:Tutorial
| Tutorial
| Please upload an image!
| Dungeon
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Difficulty | ? (edit)
| Floors | 1
| Music | Crawling, Turbo
| {"description":"","order":"0","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","difficulty":"","image":"","name":"Tutorial","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Crawling, Turbo","floors":"1"}
Floor layout of the Tutorial dungeon.
Tutorial is a dungeon in Vampire Crawlers. It is only able to accessed at the start of a new save file, and cannot be replayed after reaching the Village.
## List of encounters
All the enemies encountered in the Tutorial dungeon are variations of the  Bat. Other encounters include debris, a mana statue, and a coffin.
- One  Bat Tutorial00.
- One  Bat Tutorial01 DoubleXP and two  Bat Tutorial01.
- Destructible fallen log debris blocking the path.
- Breaking the log unlocks the  Combo Stack relic.
- Five  Bat Tutorial02.
- Destructible light source blocking the path.
- Breaking the light source grants 1 coin.
- Two rows of: One  Bat Tutorial03-5 and two  Bat Tutorial03.
- One  Bat Tutorial04-5 and seven  Bat Tutorial04.
- Destructible statue blocking the path.
- Breaking the statue grants 1 Mana slab pickup.
- Ten  Bat Tutorial04.
- A coffin with 20  Bat Tutorial05 guardians circling it.
- Defeating the guardians and opening the coffin unlocks the first Crawler character card,  Antonio.
=====

Updates are changes which affect gameplay by adding extra content or moderate to major changes of existing gameplay elements.

# Crawlers:Updates/1 million Crawlers in 1 week
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 29 April 2026.
## Hotfixes, roadmap, and other post-launch matters.
Hello,
First of all, thank you to everyone who has been playing Vampire Crawlers since launch.
The game released on April 21 on Steam, Nintendo Switch, Xbox, and PlayStation, with 1 million users in its first week. That is obviously a ridiculous number, and as our first Vampire Survivors spin-off, it means a lot to us.
Vampire Survivors began as a very small, very strange project, and over time it grew into something much bigger than we ever expected. Vampire Crawlers is the first time we've tried to take those same core ideas and push them into a completely different genre. So thank you for trusting us enough to come along for that, and for the overwhelming response.
Here's a list of hotfixes coming very soon:
- Fix a crash caused by a Gatti Amari scuffle happening as an encounter ends
- Fix a save slot error that could occur if a brand new player exited before the tutorial finished loading
- Improve detection for corrupt save data, or save data brought back from a later version to an earlier one
- Add limit to frame rate based on monitor refresh rate to to fix too many resources being used (should reduce number of crashes)
- Fix a crash caused by the Echo gem
- Demo saves still in the tutorial are now deleted when upgrading to the full game (prevents broken save states)
- Improve saving integrity to avoid corruption in the case of power cuts or unexpected app closure
- Fix an issue where two save slots could be loaded at the same time, which should help prevent achievement progress from being wiped
We are also actively working on quality of life updates, and Endless Mode.
A quick note: we generally do not want to nerf things. The goal is to fix issues, not remove the fun.
## Roadmap
We're also working on a roadmap for upcoming content. Now that players have had time to get their hands on it, we're in a much better position to decide what should come first.
So, in summary:
- Thank you for playing
- Thank you for the reviews
- Thank you for the bug reports
- Hotfixes incoming
- We have a roadmap
Thank you to everyone at Nosebleed Interactive for bringing Vampire Crawlers to life, and thank you again to everyone who has played it so far.
See you in the next news post.
=====
# Crawlers:Updates/Death Parade: Dev Update
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 28 August 2026.
## Throw away your cards.
Hello my fellow Vampire Survivors Crawlers,
I'm Marthello, your new, very serious community manager. It's nice to meet you all, but enough with the pleasantries.
My first task is to deliver a very serious update: Death Parade is taking longer than expected. We're sorry. Development is moving along, but rather than stay quiet until it's finished, we thought we'd show you some of what's on the way.
Not all of it, obviously. That's not how previews work.
## Discarding cards on purpose
Our roadmap already mentioned that Death Parade would introduce limited hand sizes and discarding.
The limit comes from a new Relic, NAME REDACTED, which caps how many cards you can hold. Simply giving you fewer cards wasn't particularly interesting, so we've been adding new ways to manage exploit a smaller hand and started turning discarded cards into something you can build around. 
For example:
## Sir Ambrojoe
Sir Ambrojoe can Switch cards, discarding random cards from your hand and immediately drawing replacements. Whenever a card is discarded, he deals damage and adds Scavenger to a random red card still in your hand.
Scavenger makes that card stronger for every card discarded during an encounter. The more you cycle your hand, the stronger your Scavengercards become. 
His weapon, La Robba, combines Discard, Scavenger and Bouncy. It rains furniture onto multiple enemies, grows stronger as you discard more cards and has a Luck-based chance to bounce between targets.
Other cards can activate through Afterlife when discarded. So yes, throwing away perfectly good cards is now an actual strategy. 
## Big Troubler
When you discard 500 cards, you unlock Big Troubler. His weapon, Unearthly Bolt, fires multiple green lightning bolts and evolves into Spirit Disturbance, a green dragon.
Playing Big Troubler discards two cards and adds two Coin Purses to your hand. While Big Troubler is active, gaining gold makes a random card free for the turn, and discarding a card gets you another Coin Purse.
He works well with any other Crawler, but he pairs especially well with Ambrojoe (hint, hint) and in Luck-heavy builds.
Both Ambrojoe and Big Troubler are examples of how we're trying to improve what we internally call "thoughtful hand management." If you think you have a better name for it, no, you don't.
## New Arcanas and Gems
Death Parade's new Gems will let you add their effects to existing cards, not just the new cards coming in Death Parade.
The patch also adds four new Arcanas:
- Infinite Combo
- Chair Game
- Crawler Brawler
- Ebb & Combo(formerly known as Unbreakable Spirit. The name was not unbreakable.)
As for what they actually do, we'll leave that for you to discover.
Thank you for your patience while we continue working on Death Parade. We'll have more to share when it's ready. 
Anyway, here's one more GIF for good measure. Enjoy.
~ Marthello
=====
# Crawlers:Updates/Death Parade: Dev Update 2
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 21 September 2026.
## There is nothing after Death.
Hello again, my fellow Vampire Survivors Crawlers,
It is I, Marthello, your very serious community manager.
First things first. Development on the mobile version of Vampire Crawlers is going well, and it's still planned for release in 2026.
But on to Death Parade. Last time, we discussed throwing away cards on purpose. This time, we're going to talk about the new stage and explain very little about why the patch is called Death Parade.
A few spoilers ahead.
## Welcome to Moongolow
Some of you already spotted it in the last post, so yes: Death Parade also adds Moongolow, a new challenge stage built around limited hands and discarding.
On your first visit, the new Relic that caps how many cards you can hold will already be active, and Moongolow will guide you through some of what Death Parade has in store. If you complete the stage once, your future visits will play a little differently. You'll also unlock the Relic as an optional toggle for Moongolow and every other stage.
Future challenge stages will follow the same rule (and yes, there will be more). Beat the challenge and its new mechanic becomes an optional toggle everywhere. Turn it on, leave it off or use it to break the game in new ways. You'll have access to all the new content either way.
Anyway, destroying a Moon Amphora might also reward you with a Switch Gem, so, as ever, property damage is encouraged.
## What is the Death Parade?
The Death Parade is here to make one thing clear: once you've beaten Red Death, there is nothing else waiting beyond it.'That's all. Enjoy the patch. We wish you and your deck the very best of luck.
## Speaking of Luck 
Death Parade also introduces three new ways for Luck to make things more inconvenient for your enemies:
- Bouncy gives attacks a Luck-based chance to bounce between enemies (requires at least two enemies).
- Crit Explosion turns critical hits into explosions. Those explosions can also crit and create more explosions.
- Roaring increases Area damage every time an attack spreads to another enemy.
For example, Big Troubler's evolved weapon, Spirit Disturbance, uses both Roaring and Crit Explosion. The dragon remains on-screen until its attack - and any chains of explosions it makes - have finished.
Thanks again for your patience as we continue working on Death Parade.
And in case you skipped directly to the end, the mobile version of Vampire Crawlers is planned for release in 2026.
We'll share more once the explosions have stopped critting.
~ Marthello
=====
# Crawlers:Updates/Death Parade: Dev Update 3
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 5 October 2026.
## Probably the final preview. Vampire Crawlers coming to mobile in October.
Hello again, my fellow Vampire Survivors Crawlers,
Marthello here, with some news.
## Vampire Crawlers is coming to mobile
That's right, Vampire Crawlers is coming to iOS and Android on October 12 at 9:00 AM BST, which is much more specific than "2026."
Soon, you'll be able to crawl dungeons, build Combos and become overpowered from the convenience of your phone.
Now, back to Death Parade. 
This time, we're looking at a new Mana card and taking a closer look at one of the new Arcanas.
## Karoma's Mana
Karoma's Mana is a new purple Mana card designed to keep your hand moving. At its base cost, it uses one Mana to Switch one card, then gives that Mana right back to you.
It also introduces Adaptability, which changes the card's cost to one more than the last card added to your Combo. The further your chain goes, the more carefully you'll need to decide where Karoma's Mana belongs.
The new Adaptability Gem lets you add the effect to existing cards, too. Death Parade's release date has already proven a little too adaptable, so from now on, we're just keeping that effect in the deck.
## Chair Game
Earlier, we revealed the names of Death Parade's four new Arcanas and didn't explain them. I've now been authorized to explain one:
Chair Game is designed for when you have an active chain, but none of the cards left in your hand can continue it.
Once per turn, it Switches up to four cards, discarding the dead ends and drawing replacements that might keep your Combo alive. Yes, even if your Combo is only x1.
Because Switch discards cards before replacing them, cards with Afterlife can still activate, cards with Scavenge (formerly Scavenger; we discarded the "r") can grow stronger and any other discard effects can also trigger. So, what looked like a failed chain can suddenly become damage, scaling and another chance to keep your Combo going.
We hope you enjoy Death Parade and the patches still to come, each with a shiny new mechanic to unlock and toggle across every stage. Use it or don't, all the new content is yours either way.
As for whether this is the final preview, there's only one way to find out.
~ Marthello

Hotfix 1.4.1 has been released some time ago, here's the change notes (Steam PC #4813, Steam Mac #4816)
- Fix for a crash caused by Gatti Amari scuffle occuring as an encounter ends
- Fix for a save slot error caused by brand new players immediately exiting game before tutorial loads
- Better detection for corrupt data, or save data being carried from a later version back to an earlier version
- Added limit to frame rate based on monitor refresh rate, to fix too many resources being used (should reduce nubmer of crashes)

- Improved saving integrity to avoid corruption in the case of power cuts or unexpected app closure
- Fix for being able to load 2 slots simultaneously
See you soon with the first QOL update and Endless mode and more detail on future Content Updates!

# Crawlers:Updates/Let's Explore Vampire Crawlers - Episode 1
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 23 January 2026.
## Master the Turboturn! Customize abilities! DEMO next month!
## Hello Vampire Survivors Crawlers!
Today we're starting a little video series to put the spotlight on the main gameplay mechanics of Vampire Crawlers, starting with a duo of: the simplest mechanic (Turboturn) and possibly the deepest one (Card Customization).
https://youtu.be/-Ael4oMaKQM
## The Turboturn!
- Turn-based combat without the wait: you don't have to pause for animations to complete before triggering your next move. The game stacks and executes your ability inputs with precision, allowing you to play at your own pace. Chaotic? Calm and thoughtful? Random-bullshit-go? Everything works, the results are accurate regardless of your preferred speed.
## Card Customisation
- Gems allow you to customize your cards with a variety of effects, ranging from simple damage multipliers, to Weapon Evolutions, to head-scratching logic twists. Whether you want a simple power boost or deep strategic planning, Gems give every player the tools to create game-breaking combos.
## The first playable demo will be available during the Steam Next Fest (and also on Xbox) on February 23rd! There will be the option to carry progress to the full game.
* While the demo is currently scheduled to launch on Steam and Xbox, the full version of Vampire Crawlers is planned to launch on Steam, Xbox, Nintendo Switch, PlayStation5, and, shortly after, mobile.
## Huge thanks to everyone for getting Vampire Crawlers to more than 200k wishlists!
Thank you all again and see you in the next news post!
- Luca
=====
# Crawlers:Updates/Let's Explore Vampire Crawlers - Episode 2 and 3
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 6 February 2026.
## Combo Multiplier, Wildcards, Characters, and Stats!
## Hello Vampire Survivors Crawlers!
Today we have a couple more episodes of Let's Explore Vampire Crawlers: one about a core gameplay mechanic, the Combo Multiplier, and another about the quirks and details of Character Selection.
## Combo Multiplier
- Play cards in ascending mana cost to increase the Combo Multiplier and therefore multiply the effectiveness of every card. Use Wildcards to bridge the gaps and create insanely long chains!
https://youtu.be/0XRi8ddiK34
## Character Selection
- Every character comes with a different starting deck and base stats. As you progress through the game, you'll be able to have up to 3 characters in your party, so that you can always bring your favourite character without having to sacrifice stat boosts.
https://youtu.be/v2PFnInWOYg
We've only scratched the surface of character selection! Stay tuned for our next update, where we dive deep into Character Cards and the mechanics of the Trigger system
## The first playable demo will be available during the Steam Next Fest on February 23rd! There will be the option to carry progress to the full game.
Thank you all again and see you in the next news post!
- Luca
=====
# Crawlers:Updates/Let's Explore Vampire Crawlers - Episode 4 and 5
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 20 February 2026.
## Character Cards! Explore Maps! Find Treasures!
## Hello Vampire Survivors Crawlers!
Today we have a couple more episodes of Let's Explore Vampire Crawlers: one about Character Card Triggers, and another about exploration.
## Character Triggers and Card types
- Like all cards, Character Cards have an immediate, straightforward effect when played. But the characters will also stay a bit longer and fight with you, with their lingering passive Triggers.
Triggers react when you play a card of their respective color, allowing you to activate all sorts of bonuses and effects. For example, by going on a crawl together with Poe, Suor Clerici, and Mortaccio, playing a simple "Armor" card will also let you draw 1 card, gain 1 Amount, and recover health!
https://youtu.be/h0SefecZ-g0
## Explore Maps, Find Treasures
- As you unlock more game mechanics, the Village will also expand, giving you new ways to spend your gold. By exiting the Village, you reach the World Map, from where you can select a new stage for your next crawl. Stage maps give you all the information you need to decide how to navigate the current floor at a glance.
Other than enemies, you'll find several points of interest: just bump into anything you want to interact with!
https://youtu.be/NtCS0XRsoto
## The first playable demo will be available during the Steam Next Fest on February 23rd! There will be the option to carry progress to the full game.
Thank you all once again and see you soon for the demo!
- Luca
=====
# Crawlers:Updates/QOL improvements update
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 23 June 2026, with the corresponding version number v1.5.
## And a more detailed content roadmap for 2026
Hello everyone!
We've finally got out our first QOL improvements patch and a detailed content roadmap for 2026. It took longer than expected because we got stuck for days trying to exit the game 🤷
Jokes aside, thank you again from everyone at poncle and Nosebleed for playing Vampire Crawlers and being patient for our updates and roadmap!
## Version 1.5 Contents
## 🔵 You can now quit the game
- Settings Button and therefore Quit options always available in the bottom left corner
- Backspace can be used to exit Shops or return to Village from the World Map
- Clearer messaging around Autosave timing added to Quit Confirm pop-up
## 🔵 Card Sorting
- Buttons to sort cards by Mana cost or Color
- Pressing up/down will quickly move your selection to the next category:
- In unsorted mode it moves to the start and end of the hand
- In colour sort mode it moves to the next colour
- In mana sort mode it moves to the next level of mana cost
## 🔵 Jeweller Clarity
- Jeweller UI now shows the chests you might find a Gem in
- Made several tweaks to make gems rarity behave in a more intutitive way (changes listed below)
- Sealing no longer requires the Player to bring a gem's frequency below common; Gems can now be also instantly sealed.
## 🔵 New Deck View
- There is now a single deck view which shows you ALL your cards in one place, and whether they are in your deck pile, your hand, or the discard pile. This is accessible from multiple screens.
## 🔵 Tooltips
- Added Tooltip Visibility options to the settings to give players more flexibility
- Removed the controller button dedicated to Tooltip toggling (it is now used for card sorting)
## 🔵 Endless Mode
- If you have completed the game, a small new teaser area is unlocked on the map. This level contains a new Relic which unlocks Endless Mode
- With the Relic active, Endless Mode is available in any dungeon that you have previously cleared.
- In Endless Mode the difficulty is increased, and you can keep digging after completing the normal floors of a dungeon, allowing you to explore further and experience even tougher encounters.
- The Jeweller is disabled in Endless Mode to make it an a challenge about going as deep as possible in a stage having to rely on the base-game RNG rather than a preset gem configuration.
## 🔵 Other changes
- Grim Grimoire is now available during level-ups
- When levelling up you will be shown if any of the cards offered are part of an evolution with your existing cards (but only if you have Evo'd that combination previously)
- On the Pause screen we now default to the bottom row of buttons to make it easier to open the Grim Grimoire (or exit…)
- Previously hidden stats like Re-roll are now shown when they are not 0
- There is now a Luck-based chance to get 4 gems per chest intead of 3
- Luck increases the chance of getting better quality gems when levelling up
## 🔵 Coin Count is still OP
- Profit.
## 🔵 Bugfixes and improvements
- Please see the list below
## 2026 content roadmap
Please note this is the minimum amount of content we're planning to release with each update. We'll actively try to pack in more QOL improvements and stuff that makes sense, especially in the later ones.
## New content strategy
As we all know, it's quite easy to become OP in Vampire Crawlers. It's a game supposed to be completed in some 20-30 hours. So this is how we're planning to keep expanding the game.
In every 2026 update, we're going to add a new post-game Challenge Stage. As the name suggests, in order to complete these stages you'll have to overcome a challenge in the form of a new game mechanic. After you emerge victorious, you'll be able to toggle On or Off that game mechanic in every stage (including the original Challenge one).
Most of the new content will be designed around the specific game mechanics introduced in the update, but obviously nothing will stop you from using those mechanics outside of the Challenge and find new ways to break the base game.
As you can see from the roadmap, the challenge mechanic in the next update will be a limited hand size, paired with a staple mechanic of card games: discarding.
The idea is that by the end of the 2026 update cycle you'll be able to decide to play the game in a more challenging way or stick to the base version, while still getting all the added content and mechanics.
## A tease about 2027
For the next year, we are planning something different than Challenge Stages: we are building the Astral Stair.
Where will you go? Who will you be?
## Steam Community Items are out today
- 1 Badge (5 levels + foil)
- 5 Trading Cards (Featuring Gennaro, Mortaccio, Poppea, Imelda, Clerici)
- 4 Profile Backgrounds
- 5 Emoticons
Here's a preview of one of the cards:
(If you are not sure what any of that means, you can read how to unlock Steam Community Items here.)
## Other platforms
This update will reach all Consoles soon!
There will be a couple of major changes for Nintendo Switch 2:
- Game is now 1080p in handheld mode, and 4K when docked
- Mouse: the stick on the controller that acts as the mouse is now functional for dungeon navigation
## Bugfixes and improvements
- Gems which move down in rarity (e.g Rare to common) will appear in both their original chest and current rarity chest. Gems which move up in rarity will be removed from their original Chests.
This helps reduce the chance of finding a gem which has been made rarer but allows high-quality gems (like the Evo Gem) to appear more often when rarity has been reduced.
- The gems which drop from different chests have been adjusted:
- Bronze: Common, Uncommon, Rare (unchanged)
- Silver: Rare, Epic, Legendary (Small chance of legendary)
- Gold: Epic, Legendary (unchanged)
- UI arrows added to the Jeweller for consistency with other Shops
- Scroll views in the Jeweller should work more consistently
- Fixed overflow bug with coin amount and enemies'health going negative
- Fix for Gorgeous Moon only targeting the front row
- Fix for Overkill UI getting stuck if rapidly re-triggered
- Fix for crash when rearranging cards in hand as the turn was ending
- Fix for ghost UI buttons showing on Credits when using mouse. This one's for you Alex!
- Save Data time stamps are now shown in local time (rather than UTC used previously)
- End of Floor shovel now animates onto the screen instead of just appearing
- You can only revive if you've actually died, not when completing a dungeon
- Cards affected by Leader or Midas gems now show an indicator that this is happening
- A card with the right colour, which has the right colour gem inserted, can now unlock the Silver Chest by itself; any card with the rainbow gem can now unlock a Silver Chest
- Game now correctly respects the target framerate set in the display options (Unless Power Saving Mode is enabled, in which case framerate is locked to 30fps)
- Audio
- Extra attacks which happen at the end of a turn (e.g. Santa Water) now play their sounds correctly
- Shops should now pay their Buy/Fail sounds correctly
- Various small fixes to Drowner audio
- Localisation updates
- Clearer Quit behaviour (see above)
- Equip button label in Inn changed to "Add"
- Gem Description updates for Drain and Mug gems to improve clarity: Enemies that get hit by these gems will do the effect when they die and do not need to be killed at the moment the gem hits them
- Gem "Easy Combo" renamed to "Combo Breaker", with improved text descriptions that better match the way it works (players were not understanding that the combo would end when this gem is used)
- General bug fixing and minor polish
=====
# Crawlers:Updates/Thank you for over 170k wishlist additions!
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 16 December 2025.
Vampire Crawlers has already got more than 170k wishlist on Steam, so thank you very much to all players for your trust!
So far we have published only what is effectively a hype trailer to try to convey how it feels to play Vampire Crawlers, with a few sneak peeks at many of the game features and options to snowball into unhinged chaos. So, for a first update, we are showing a new gameplay trailer and some simple and unedited gameplay footage of the very first dungeon floor, focused on basic gameplay.
This should let you see what is at the core of Vampire Crawlers: have fun playing satisfying abilities in turbo turn-based battles, at whatever speed you prefer.
In the following weeks we'll also talk about all other gameplay features. From basic stuff, like why and how we are moving in dungeon floors instead of menus, to how easily, but also deeply, we can customise cards and abilities. Please know that beyond this facade of casual approach to the genre, people who are into min/maxing and total optimization will actually be able to dramatically customise the RNG!
But enough spoilers, let's start unwrapping the features of Vampire Crawlers starting with some unedited gameplay footage (alpha footage subject to change in the final version; mouse cursor not included :p )
## Gameplay video:
https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SARs48njusw
## Unedited gameplay footage:
https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=U60hjHh-Nuc
Fans of Vampire Survivors might also be interested in knowing that all enemies and icons are getting a fresh look in Vampire Crawlers, always made by the incredible Glauber Kotaki! Here's a little preview:
Finally, a lot of players have been asking for a demo! We will make one, but we don't have a date for it yet, we'll keep you updated through the Steam News and on Discord as we keep working on the game.
Thank you all again and see you in the next news post!
- Luca

# Crawlers:Updates/Thank you for playing the Vampire Crawlers Demo!
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 6 March 2026.
## Steam Next Fest is over. Back to crawling.
Hello Vampire Survivors Crawlers,
Steam Next Fest is over, which means I can stop refreshing the stats page to check the numbers quite so often.
Thanks to everyone who tried the Vampire Crawlers demo during the event! Whether you played for five minutes or five hours, streamed it, reported bugs, or shared feedback, we appreciate it!
Watching players jump in and immediately try to break things remains one of the most useful parts of releasing a demo. You kept crawling, and the game mostly behaved.
Here's a quick look at how things went.
## Demo stats
During the Steam Next Fest, the demo got:
358k downloads
274k players
That's a lot of people trying the game in a short amount of time!
Vampire Crawlers has now also passed 420,000+ wishlists.
Thank you for giving it a go!
## FAQs
Will demo progress carry over to the full game?
Yes. You'll have the option to carry your demo progress into the full game at launch.
If you prefer starting fresh though, that's also possible: this game has save slots from day one😅
What is a TurboTurn and why should I care?
Turn-based games can often be a bit slow because of the waiting between actions. TurboTurn is our attempt to remove that waiting.
You can queue and play cards as quickly as you want, while the game resolves everything correctly in the background.
I got soft-locked after a boss fight / the battle didn't end.
This showed up a few times during Next Fest. It's a bug and it's already being addressed for the full release.
Thanks to everyone who reported it and included details.
When is the release date?
We're very close to choosing a release date, since we already have all the content we had planned for launch, and have been bugfixing and polishing for a while!
We are planning to launch on Steam and consoles on the same day.
Are you taking the demo down now?
No. The demo will stay available between now and launch, and likely afterwards too.
Why does the game feel so easy?
The demo is intentionally a bit forgiving so players can experiment with the systems.
The full game will ask a bit more from you.
## Help, what even is this game?
If you want a quick overview of the game mechanics, we made a short video series called Let's Explore Vampire Crawlers:
EP1: TurboTurn basics and Card Customisation https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-Ael4oMaKQM
EP2: Combo multiplier and Wildcards https://youtu.be/0XRi8ddiK34
EP3: Party Selection and Stats https://youtu.be/v2PFnInWOYg
EP4: Character Triggers and Card Types https://youtu.be/h0SefecZ-g0
EP5: Village, World Map, and Floor Maps https://youtu.be/NtCS0XRsoto
EP6: Blacksmith and Jeweller https://youtu.be/BssTmgvyiVo
EP7: Arcana Game Modifiers https://youtu.be/IqT1n125Oys
Well, that's it for now.
See you in the next news post!
Luca

## Vampire Crawlers
## v1.4.1 Patch Notes [Steam PC & Mac]
- Fix a crash caused by a Gatti Amari scuffle happening as an encounter ends
- Fix a save slot error that could occur if a brand new player exited before the tutorial finished loading
- Improve detection for corrupt save data, or save data brought back from a later version to an earlier one
- Add limit to frame rate based on monitor refresh rate to to fix too many resources being used (should reduce number of crashes)
- Fix a crash caused by the Echo gem
- Demo saves still in the tutorial are now deleted when upgrading to the full game (prevents broken save states)
- Improve saving integrity to avoid corruption in the case of power cuts or unexpected app closure
- Fix an issue where two save slots could be loaded at the same time, which should help prevent achievement progress from being wiped

# Crawlers:Updates/Vampire Crawlers Demo Update - Steam & Xbox
This official news post is copied verbatim from the Discord server.
It was posted on 30 June 2026, with the corresponding version number v1.5.
## Vampire Crawlers Demo Update - Steam & Xbox
This patch includes all the previous fixes in the Hot Fix update that was previously released on Steam
- Small adjustments to UI to make text clearer on smaller screens
- Fix for a save slot error caused by brand new players immediately exiting game before the tutorial loads
- Better detection for corrupt data, or save data being carried from a later version back to an earlier version
- Added limit to frame rate based on monitor refresh rate, to fix too many resources being used to help reduce number of crashes
- Fix for a crash caused by the Echo gem
- Demo saves that were in the tutorial are now deleted when upgrading to main game to prevent broken save states
- Improved saving integrity to avoid corruption in the case of power cuts or unexpected app closure
- Fix for being able to load 2 slots simultaneously, to help prevent the issue where all achievement progress was wiped
General
- Card Sorting
- Implemented two sorting modes: By Mana, and By Colour
- Tap the appropriate button to sort your hand using the current sorting method
- Pressing up/down will quickly move your selection to the next category:
- In unsorted mode it moves to the start and end of the hand
- In colour sort mode it moves to the next colour
- In mana sort mode it moves to the next level of mana cost
- New Deck View
- Single deck view which shows you ALL your cards in one place, and whether they're in your deck pile, your hand, or discard pile
- Removed the button to only show the discard pile
- UI has been tidied up to make better use of space, and to scroll correctly
Tooltips
- Added Tooltip Visibility options to the settings to allow more flexibility
- Removed the controller button dedicated to Tooltip toggling, now used for card sorting
Settings/Quitting
- Pause Button has been replaced with a Settings Button in bottom left corner
- Settings screen can now be opened in the Village or on the World Map without navigating to the Graveyard location
- Clearer messaging around Autosave timing added to Quit Confirm pop-up
- Changes to options/settings should save reliably
- and a fix for a rare crash when changing settings
- PC: Backspace can be used to exit Shops or return to Village from the World Map
- PC: Quit to Desktop button added on platforms that support it
- - Other Quality of Life changes**
- Grim Grimoire is now available during level-ups
- When levelling up you will be shown if any of the cards offered are part of an evolution with your existing cards *(but only if you have Evo’d that combination previously)*
- On the Pause screen it now defaults to the bottom row of buttons to make it easier to open the Grim Grimoire *(or exit…)*
- Previously hidden stats like Re-roll are now shown when they are not 0
Other
- Game now correctly respects the target framerate set in the display options
- Unless Power Saving Mode is enabled, in which case framerate is locked to 30fps
- Fix for crash when rearranging cards in hand as the turn was ending
- Fix for ghost UI buttons showing on Credits when using mouse. *This one’s for you Alex!* <:MachevuoiVS:1130828916165197924>
- Save Data time stamps are now shown in local time *(rather than UTC used previously)*
- End of Floor shovel now animates onto the screen instead of just appearing
- You can only revive if you’ve actually died, not when completing a dungeon
- Localisation updates
- Clearer Quit behaviour *(see above)*
- Equip button label in Inn changed to “Add”
- Audio
- Extra attacks which happen at the end of a turn *(e.g. Santa Water)* now play their sounds correctly
- Shops should now pay their Buy/Fail sounds correctly
- Various small fixes to The Drowner audio
- General bug fixing and minor polish

# Crawlers:Updates/Vampire Crawlers is coming April 21st
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 19 March 2026.
## Buy a Vampire Crawlers for 9.99 money
Hello Vampire Survivors Crawlers,
Today we have yet another trailer.
It has no gameplay, but includes the price, a release date, and a few other things that may not be helpful. Unless you were wondering what floor chicken costs.
You can watch it here:
https://youtu.be/jaAEKYGnxrA
I apologise for the incomprehensible reference-inception but just a little
## Vampire Crawlers will cost £9.99 / $9.99 / €9.99 / ¥1200
## You can get it for Steam, Nintendo Switch, Xbox, and PS5 starting April 21st
The game is coming to mobile a bit later, but still in 2026.
You can wishlist the game by clicking the link below.
https://store.steampowered.com/app/3265700/
If you want to see how the game works, the demo is still available too.
https://store.steampowered.com/app/4329470/
Thank you all and see you in the next news post!
- Luca
## FAQs
When does the game release?
April 21st 2026
How much does it cost?
£9.99 / $9.99 / €9.99 / ¥1200
The price might be slightly different on some consoles due to platform restrictions.
My currency isn't listed, how much will it be?
For regional pricing, we tried to price the game using real purchasing power rather than relying on suggested conversion rates. Hopefully we got it right, if somehow we messed up and the game is outrageously expensive rather than cheap, please let us know when it launches!
Why is this more expensive than Vampire Survivors?
VS was lightning in a bottle, originally made by a solo dev (hello!) with a budget of £1100 and grew in content and production value over time thanks to the overwhelming success and support from the players.
Vampire Crawlers instead is made from scratch by the teams of poncle and Nosebleed Interactive, which makes it a more traditional and much more costly production.
As mentioned a few times in past Steam News, we price our stuff depending on its production value and by keeping affordability in mind.
What platforms is it coming to?
Vampire Crawlers will launch on Steam, Nintendo Switch, Xbox, and PlayStation. Mobile versions are planned for a bit later, but still in 2026.
Does the game support controllers?
Yes. Controller support is included.
Does the game support Cross Save between different platforms?
Not at launch, we're looking into it on time for the mobile ports.
Will Save Data carry over on Steam if I play the demo?
Yes. We advise to not overdo it with the demo, but who are we to stop you?
How will the game differ from the demo?
Other than obviously having all of the content, the game will launch with a lot of fixes and improvements compared to the demo (and thanks to everyone that helped by reporting bugs, feedback, and suggestions!). The demo will also be updated later on to reflect the new standard, as we are planning to keep it available for download.

# Crawlers:Updates/Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 21 April 2026.
## Out now on Steam, Nintendo Switch, Xbox, and PlayStation
Hello Vampire S̶u̶r̶v̶i̶v̶o̶r̶s̶ Crawlers!
The time has come. Today we're releasing Vampire Crawlers, the first spin-off of Vampire Survivors, now available on Steam, Nintendo Switch, Xbox, and PlayStation.
With Vampire Crawlers, we wanted to take the snowballing chaos of Vampire Survivors and redesign it as a TurboTurn™-based, card-driven BLOBBER with roguelite elements. One moment, you're taking a stroll through the average Italian countryside; the next, you're exploring infested dungeons, building busted decks, and headbutting objects at alarming speed, as God intended.
You can watch the launch trailer below.
https://youtu.be/2uBGewEqOVg
**Fun fact: Vampire Crawlers contains exactly the same number of vampires as Vampire Survivors. 
## Vampire Crawlers
If you played the demo, the full game expands on those systems with more content, plus fixes and improvements across the board. The game is built around:
- TurboTurn™ combat:Turn-based combat without the wait. Queue your moves instantly and let the game resolve them at full speed.
- The Combo:cards are played in ascending mana order, and each step multiplies the next card's effect.
- Wild cardsthat let you extend the stack and push your turns to 10, 20, 30… and possibly more than that
- Deckbuilding through progression: gain new cards, find power-ups, chase weapon evolutions, and shape your build as you go
- Character cards and summoned survivors that add more effects and help create larger cascades
- Dungeon crawling:explore dungeons full of treasures, floor progression, interactive points of interest, and something seldom seen in Vampire Survivors: functioning walls
- A pace you choose yourself: take your time and be tactical, or play turns as fast as you humanly can
The full version also comes with the fixes and improvements made after the demo feedback period, and we're planning to keep the demo available.
Thank you again to everyone who took the time to play, report bugs, and send feedback.
## Releasing now — $9.99 / €9.99 / £9.99
You can get Vampire Crawlers on Steam by clicking the link below.
https://store.steampowered.com/app/3265700/Vampire_Crawlers_The_Turbo_Wildcard_from_Vampire_Survivors/
## The Music of Vampire Crawlers
You can now experience crawling through the average Italian countryside at your own pace with the Vampire Crawlers Original Soundtrack!
Featuring music by the legendary Yoko Shimomura, Daniele Zandara, Filippo Vicarelli, and Michael Coviello.
The OST contains:
- Over 1 hour of music from Vampire Crawlers
- MP3 and FLAC (lossless) music files
- 13 main tracks with 13 variations
- 5 jingles
You can listen to it here for the low, low price of nothing:
https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=s4zN7PtCWsA&list=PLOt3Kp6xo5ohIP0SBdsDtTC-ExS3o682p&index=1
Or download it here once you get the game:
https://store.steampowered.com/app/4564300/Vampire_Crawlers_Soundtrack/
That's all for now. Thanks for staying with us, and happy crawling!
-From everyone at poncle and Nosebleed Interactive
=====
# Crawlers:Updates/VAMPIRE CRAWLERS: the turbo wildcard from VAMPIRE SURVIVORS
This official news post is copied verbatim from Steam (archived from the original).
It was posted on 20 November 2025.
## We just announced a first spin-off of Vampire Survivors
https://youtu.be/JbDVqLlLarY
After years of tiny teasers, we're finally announcing VAMPIRE CRAWLERS: the turbo wildcard from VAMPIRE SURVIVORS!
As a candidate for most stupid game name of the year the minute it got announced, VAMPIRE CRAWLERS has the goal to bring many of the elements of Vampire Survivors to an existing game genre: deckbuilding. Or was it dungeon crawling? Blobber maybe? Eh.
Play cards in a turbo turn-based combat,
Explore dungeon maps to find treasures and customisations,
All dressed in a special Vampire Survivors sauce and some usual little rogue-lite elements.
https://store.steampowered.com/app/3265700/Vampire_Crawlers_The_Turbo_Wildcard_from_Vampire_Survivors
VAMPIRE CRAWLERS will arrive in 2026 and is the first of our concepts for VS spin-offs that we've been making with other indie studios! It's a full game that we're nuts enough to aim to globally release on all platforms next year: please wish us luck!
We at poncle hope that you'll like VAMPIRE CRAWLERS just as much as we do: both making it and play testing it has been an absolute blast for both deckbuilding veterans and people who never played a card game before.
See you closer to the end of the year for our 2025 recap and a glance at future content for Vampire Survivors (and not only), but in the meantime please let us know what you think of VAMPIRE CRAWLERS so far and also if you have suggestions that can beat the full official title:
VAMPIRE CRAWLERS: the turbo wildcard from VAMPIRE SURVIVORS!
---
## FAQs
- How did this project start?
Shortly after the initial success of Vampire Survivors, I was already thinking of getting in touch with other indie studios to start working on spin-offs, so that I could focus on building my team to keep working on VS, while also getting some of my game concepts for existing genres to be developed. This means that it is something that has effectively been in the works for almost 4 years! I've been calling this kind of projects "experiments" in the past Steam news.
- Is this some sort of sequel to Vampire Survivors?
This is just a spin-off of Vampire Survivors, hopefully the first of a series of spin-offs! The idea is to take some of the accidental core pillars behind the development of VS and to apply them to existing genres. I'm talking about accessibility, immediacy, affordability, replayability, lightheartedness, and tons of flashy stuff.
- Why is this a "Vampire Survivors" game and not a new IP?
VS is very well known, so I hope that this will help players understand immediately that this game has been made with the same core principles in mind, simply applied to a different game genre.
Also this helps massively getting out of the way one of the major blockers I always had when making games: defining story, settings, lore etc:
When making VS, I managed to put all of that aside and let it all come out organically while developing the game, by taking inspiration just from a weapon icon, a music track (from the usual VS composers Daniele Zandara and Filippo Vicarelli, who are back, covering each other's music tracks for Vampire Crawlers), or even wacky theories from the community! Now all that lore has a huge value, it allows me and the other devs to just focus on the gameplay aspects of the game, while at the same time allowing space for new ideas thanks to the open-endedness of VS.
- Will you update Vampire Crawlers post-launch like Vampire Survivors?
Yes, we are already planning to keep supporting the game post-launch and we have a thousand ideas about what we could do! Vampire Crawlers is a whole game, with a beginning and an end, so we are going to wait and see what the players say and suggest before choosing what to do post-launch.
- When is it releasing?
When almost every other game is releasing: before GTA6 :)
We're aiming for early/mid 2026
- So, is this a deckbuilder dungeon crawler?
As I'm getting similar questions so I shall re-post. VC is not a deckbuild game. Could move subjectively but not a dungeon crawler game either. By incorporating with the concept of turbo(turning), it's totally brand new genre called action Turboturn™ game(Turbo Turn system).
(YES, it's just a rogue-lite deckbuilder with a touch of dungeon crawling)
- Is this a new game from Luca Galante?
Yes, hello, the game concepts for the VS spin-off are born from the same Italian-memes-ridden mind, then expertly developed, improved, and brought to life by the teams partner of poncle
=====

# Crawlers:Version history
Version history
Version history documents key major content updates in a concise listing. For a complete update history, see Updates. 
## Patch 1.0 - 21 April 2026
Patch Notes: TBA
In version 1.0, the following content was available in the game:
22 Character Cards:
40 Attack Cards:
7 Defense Cards:
19 Stat Boost Cards:
6 Mana Cards:
14 Wild Cards:
10 Dungeons:
16 Relics:
x Enemies:
58 Gems:
12 Arcanas:

Vicious Hunger

| {"type":"attack","cost":"2","id":"Card_A_2_ViciousHunger","unlocked by":"Evolving Gatti Amari.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","evofromgroup":"Stone Mask","evofrom":"Gatti Amari","image":"","name":"Vicious Hunger","max gem slots":"2","release":"21 April 2026","gem slots":"1","text":"Deal 100 damage. May turn enemies into coins.","demo":"No"}
Vicious Hunger is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers.
It is evolved from  Gatti Amari with any card in the  Stone Mask group and an  Evolution gem.
- On usage, Vicious Hunger applies a smoke-cloud visual on the damaged enemies which remains until defeat. Any enemy with this status-effect visual has a chance of being turned into gold coins once defeated (including bosses).
=====
# Crawlers:Village
| Village
| The village of Gorton, a sleepy, parochial place. Don't let its tranquil exterior fool you. This is a melting pot for heroes and heroines! An adventurer's terminus quo.
| Dungeon
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Difficulty | N/A
| Floors | N/A
| Music | Never Too Prepared
| {"description":"The village of Gorton, a sleepy, parochial place. Don't let its tranquil exterior fool you. This is a melting pot for heroes and heroines! An adventurer's terminus quo.","order":"1","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","difficulty":"N/A","image":"","name":"Village","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Never Too Prepared","floors":"N/A"}
Gorton, also known as the Village, is the main hub of Vampire Crawlers where multiple buildings for various mechanics are present. It is unlocked after completing the Tutorial.
## List of buildings
| Menu item | Building name | Description | Unlocked by
| Unlocks | Town Hall | Track your Unlocks. | Completing the Tutorial.
| Settings | Settings | A Graveyard seems the most appropriate place for an Options menu. | Completing the Tutorial.
| Crawlers | Inn | The Gorton Bell Inn. Relax with a pint of milk and ask mighty Crawlers to join your party. | Completing Floor 1 in Mad Forest.
| Arcana | Fortune Teller | Step right up for all things Arcana related. | Obtaining the Polentír.
| World Map | World Map | Venture forth with your party and Arcana. Adventure awaits! | Completing the Tutorial.
| Blacksmith | Blacksmith | In the Blacksmith you can modify Cards and Gems. | Obtaining the Stardust Anvil.
| Relics | Museum | The Museum of antiquities. Here you can peruse a collection of Relics. | Playing two dungeons.
| Power Ups | Shop | Purchase Power Ups to boost your stats. Got some rare things on sale, stranger! | Collecting 250 coins.
Blacksmith, Arcana/Fortune Teller, and Relics/Museum were introduced with the full game, while the rest of the options listed above were released in the demo.
=====

| {"type":"attack","cost":"0","id":"Card_A_0_Whip","text":"Deal 8 damage to multiple enemies.","unlock":"Available by default.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","evointo":"Bloody Tear","image":"","name":"Whip","max gem slots":"2","release":"21 April 2026","gem slots":"1","demo":"Yes","evointogroup":"Hollow Heart"}
Whip is an attack card in Vampire Crawlers. It is present in  Antonio's starting deck. It starts with 1 gem slot and is unlocked by default. The minimum level required for this card to appear in level up reward selection is 1.
It can be evolved into  Bloody Tear with any card in the  Hollow Heart group and an  Evolution gem.
## Effects
Whip has a base  Amount of 1, with each projectile dealing a base Damage of 8 to every enemy which does not have another enemy in front of it.

# Crawlers:Wiki
Vampire Survivors Wiki
## Welcome to the Vampire Crawlers wiki
 This is the official wiki for Vampire Crawlers, a casual turn-based deckbuilder with roguelite elements. Build busted decks, explore familiar dungeons from a new perspective, and unleash world‑ending combos.
 Vampire Crawlers is available to play across Xbox Series S|X, Steam, PlayStation 5, and Nintendo Switch since the release on 21 April 2026.

# Crawlers:Wild
This article is about the gem. For the card type, see Crawlers:Wild cards.
| Wild
| 
| Gem
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| In demo | No
| Rarity | Ultra Rare
| Card text | Makes the card Wild.
| Unlocked by | Playing 100 Wild cards.
| {"unlocked by":"Playing 100 Wild cards.","image":"","name":"Wild","rarity":"Ultra Rare","release":"21 April 2026","text":"Makes the card Wild.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","demo":"No"}
Wild is a gem in Vampire Crawlers. This gem will make the card, that it is applied to, have some properties of a wild card. A couple of changes in this would include:
- The removal of the mana cost of the card that it is on (or, effectively, its mana cost becomes 0);
- The ability to connect two separate combos (after usage, any card can be used for the next part of the combo, though the player should aim for the one(s) with the lowest mana cost for maximizing the combo).
=====
# Crawlers:Wild Buff
| Wild Buff
| Cards picked up from light sources now benefit from combo multipliers.
| Arcana
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Card text | Cards picked up from light sources get combo multipliers.
| Unlocked by | Playing 200 cards picked up from light sources.
| {"image":"","description":"Cards picked up from light sources now benefit from combo multipliers.","unlocked by":"Playing 200 cards picked up from light sources.","release":"21 April 2026","text":"Cards picked up from light sources get combo multipliers.","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","name":"Wild Buff"}
Wild Buff is an arcana in Vampire Crawlers. When active, wild cards will be buffed by the current combo multiplier. This includes wild cards that aren't picked up from light sources, but were generated by other cards, such as those with a  Coin Card gem slotted into them.
=====
# Crawlers:Wild cards
Wild cards are grey-colored cards in Vampire Crawlers which can be used at any point without breaking a Combo.
Most are single-use cards that are destroyed once played (unless slotted with Crawlers:Fireproof).
And at the moment, Bracelet is the only vanilla (/natural / non-Wild-gem slotted) Wild card to scale with the combo multiplier.
Primary benefit of Wild-cards is to allow the next card to continue the combo regardless of mana cost. This, for example, allows players to return back to 0 (zero-)mana cost and resume stacking consecutively upwards. Or even allow players to disregard the numerical chain without breaking the combo (E.G. a chain of 3-Wild-6-Wild-5-Wild-Wild-1-Wild-0). 
## List of wild cards

# Crawlers:World Map
| World Map
| Venture forth with your party and Arcana. Adventure awaits!
| Building
| Release | 21 April 2026 (Update)
| Function | Allows for selecting and accessing a Dungeon.
| Unlocking | Completing the Tutorial.
| Music | Never Too Prepared (World Map)
| Chronology
| ← Previous | Current | Next →
| Fortune Teller | World Map | Blacksmith
| {"description":"Venture forth with your party and Arcana. Adventure awaits!","next":"Blacksmith","update":"Vampire Crawlers, the first-ever Vampire Survivors spin-off, is here!","function":"Allows for selecting and accessing a Dungeon.","image":"","name":"World Map","prev":"Fortune Teller","release":"21 April 2026","music":"Never Too Prepared (World Map)","unlocking":"Completing the Tutorial."}
The World Map is one of the eight areas that can be found in the Village. It allows for the player to select and access a specific Dungeon. It is unlocked after completing the Tutorial.
Players can navigate to the World Map option in the Village quicker by pressing Backspace. Pressing that key while in the World Map will lead back to the Village option within the World Map.
If the player has the  Sorceress' Tears or the  Seventh Trumpet, they can enable or disable their respective modes, Hurry Mode and Endless Mode, through the World Map or the Museum.
## List of dungeons
See Crawlers:Dungeons
## Trivia
- This is one of the three areas, within the Village, that are visible after completing the Tutorial, with the other being the Town Hall and the Graveyard.
- Before the 1.5 update, the player could press Esc in the Village to instantly access the World Map, and pressing it in the World Map would instantly access the Village.
